### [1] [A Control-Theoretic Approach for Resource-Aware Consensus in Multi-Agent AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25099)
*James Flagg,Esteban A. Hernandez-Vargas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将LLM多智能体系统的集体信念动态建模为离散时间切换系统的方法，引入计算预算与共识安全集的概念，推导出共识时间与token成本的显式界，并构建共识-预算证书区域保证有限时间收敛且不耗尽资源，同时建立自适应拓扑切换在收敛速度与通信成本之间权衡的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统依赖智能体间通信解决复杂推理任务，但关于共识性能与计算资源之间关系的严谨保证仍然缺乏。

Method: 将集体信念动态表征为离散时间切换系统，其中通信拓扑具有不同的共识收缩速率和token成本；通过将剩余计算预算增广到信念动态中，定义联合捕获一致性与资源可行性的共识安全集；构建共识-预算证书区域。

Result: 推导出共识时间和token支出的显式界；建立了自适应拓扑切换在收敛速度与通信成本之间权衡的条件；数值实验和实际LLM-MAS部署验证了预测的共识-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 控制理论证书能够实现AI系统中的资源感知协调，为LLM多智能体系统提供有限时间收敛且不资源耗尽的保证。

Abstract: Large language model multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) rely on inter-agent communication to solve complex reasoning tasks, yet rigorous guarantees relating consensus performance to computational resources remain limited. Here, we present a novel way to characterize collective belief dynamics as a discrete-time switched system in which communication topologies have distinct consensus-contraction rates and token costs. By augmenting the belief dynamics with the remaining computational budget, we define a consensus safe set that jointly captures agreement and resource feasibility. We derive explicit bounds on consensus time and token expenditure and construct a consensus-budget certificate region guaranteeing finite-time convergence without resource exhaustion. We further establish conditions under which adaptive topology switching achieves a trade-off between convergence speed and communication cost relative to fixed-topology strategies. Numerical experiments and live LLM-MAS deployments show the predicted consensus-cost trade-offs, demonstrating how control-theoretic certificates can enable resource-aware coordination in AI systems.

</details>


### [2] [Barycentric Weak Inner-Product Gromov-Wasserstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25145)
*Youssef Mroueh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）框架，用于处理一对多（one-to-many）场景下的分布比较问题。通过比较源空间关系与目标条件分布关系（而非逐点关系），引入了重心弱内积GW（wIGW），并证明了其等价于在均值保持展宽约束下搜索中间目标几何的最优化问题。文章还给出了有限支撑下的迭代算法、凸-凹性分析和收敛界，并通过点云、图特征细化及PBMC多组学实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Gromov-Wasserstein距离通过逐点关系比较分布，在一对多设定下过于敏感——当多个目标结果细化同一个源状态、且其均值承载了几何结构时，逐点比较会失真。作者希望引入弱化比较方式，使比较发生在源关系与目标条件分布关系之间，从而更稳健地处理这种结构化不匹配。

Method: 提出重心弱内积GW（wIGW），比较源关系与由耦合诱导的目标条件均值的关系。关键贡献是证明wIGW²(μ,ν) = inf_{η⪯_{cx}ν} IGW²(μ,η)，其中η⪯_{cx}ν表示ν是η的均值保持展宽。在有限二阶矩条件下，利用鞅拼接恢复最优耦合。引入岭正则化后，通过矩对偶将问题转化为A-B min-max博弈：内层为带二次代价的弱最优传输，外层优化矩阵A和B。对有限支撑测度给出迭代算法，在定量岭条件下证明约简问题是凸-凹的，且投影外迭代对非精确内层求解满足显式收缩界。

Result: 理论结果表明均值保持的目标细化可以产生零代价；实验上，点云和图特征细化任务验证了该性质。在配对PBMC多组学研究中，通过与细胞到细胞（one-to-one）设定对比，展示了原型到细胞（prototype to cell，即一对多）设定在RNA/ATAC对齐的基于图谱的细胞类型转移中优于或匹敌传统方法。

Conclusion: wIGW框架通过允许目标测度被均值保持地细化，克服了标准GW在一对多场景下的过敏感性痛点。算法具有收敛保证，实验验证了其对现实生物数据（如多组学细胞类型映射）的有效性。该方法为结构化分布对齐提供了新的理论工具和实用算法。

Abstract: Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) compares distributions through relations within each space. This pointwise comparison can be too sensitive in one-to-many settings, where several target outcomes refine one source state and their mean carries the geometry of interest. We introduce a weak GW framework that compares source relations with relations between the target conditional laws induced by a coupling. For inner-product relations, we retain the conditional means $m_π(x)=\mathbb{E}_π[Y\mid X=x]$. The resulting barycentric weak inner-product GW (wIGW) satisfies $\mathrm{wIGW}_{\mathrm{bar}}^2(μ,ν)=\inf_{η\preceq_{\mathrm{cx}}ν}\mathrm{IGW}^2(μ,η)$. Here $η\preceq_{\mathrm{cx}}ν$ means that $ν$ is a mean-preserving spread of $η$. Thus wIGW searches for an intermediate target geometry that can be refined into the prescribed target law without changing conditional means. Under finite second moments, minimizers exist and martingale gluing recovers an optimal coupling. With ridge regularization, moment duality gives an $A$-$B$ min-max problem whose inner step is weak optimal transport with a quadratic cost parameterized by $A$ and $B$; the outer problem optimizes these matrices. For finitely supported measures, we give an iterative algorithm. Under a quantitative ridge condition, the reduced problem is convex--concave, and the projected outer iteration satisfies an explicit contraction bound for inexact inner solves. Point cloud and graph feature refinement experiments illustrate how mean-preserving target refinements can have zero cost. A paired peripheral blood mononuclear cell (PBMC) multiome study evaluates atlas based cell type transfer through RNA/ATAC alignment in cell to cell and prototype to cell settings, with the prototype to cell setting representing the one-to-many case.

</details>


### [3] [Optimization of Closed-Loop Pumped-Storage Hydropower Siting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25175)
*Luiz Rodolpho Albuquerque,Rafael Kelman,Tarcisio Castro,Ana Petrungaro,Tiago Andrade*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍HERA-S计算建模框架，用于简化闭式抽水蓄能（PSH）站点的区域勘察与预可行性规划，并在巴西里约热内卢案例研究中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变可再生能源的大规模并网带来运行挑战，频繁弃电凸显了对长时储能的需求。同时，传统水电开发受社会-环境约束限制，而闭式抽水蓄能因不依赖自然河道成为可行替代方案，但缺乏标准化、自动化的选址评估工具。

Method: 提出HERA-S框架，由PSR开发，集成空间筛选、坝体优化、成本估算和社会-环境影响评分等模块，采用整数规划模型优化坝体填筑与开挖土方平衡，并考虑库区间距离约束。

Result: 框架应用于巴西里约热内卢案例，实现了自动化选址、坝体优化与成本评估，显著提升了预可行性规划的敏捷性。

Conclusion: HERA-S提供了闭式抽水蓄能选址的标准化工具体系，可加速长时储能项目的前期评估，支持可再生能源并网下的电网灵活性需求。

Abstract: Accelerating variable renewable energy integration introduces substantial operational challenges to modern power grids. Frequent curtailment of surplus renewable generation highlights the pressing need for long-duration energy storage capable of shifting generation to high-demand periods while providing essential ancillary services and grid inertia. Socio-environmental constraints increasingly limit conventional hydropower development; however, closed-loop pumped-storage hydropower (PSH) presents a viable alternative because its off-river reservoirs avoid natural watercourses. This paper introduces HERA-S, a computational modeling framework developed by PSR (supported by EDF, CTG, Brookfield, and Rio Light) to streamline and standardize regional PSH site prospecting. HERA-S automates spatial screening, dam optimization, cost estimation, and socio-environmental impact scoring. Applied to a case study in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, the framework employs an integer programming model to optimize dam-fill and excavation mass balances against inter-reservoir distance, significantly improving pre-feasibility planning agility.

</details>


### [4] [Approximating Energy-Regulation Feasible Region of Virtual Power Plants: A Data-driven Inverse Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25248)
*Ruike Lyu,Hongye Guo,Qixin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的方法来近似虚拟电厂（VPP）的能量-调节可行域（FR），采用虚拟电池模型并通过基于原始运行模型生成的多场景运行数据、利用逆向优化确定模型参数，数值实验验证了该方法的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FR聚合方法依赖解析方法，在通用性和适应性方面存在问题，难以有效处理VPP内部分布式能源资源（DERs）的个体运行模型差异。

Method: 提出数据驱动的FR近似方法：采用虚拟电池模型近似VPP的聚合FR，通过逆向优化基于原始运行模型生成的多场景运行数据来确定虚拟电池模型参数。

Result: 数值测试验证了所提方法的准确性，表明该方法能有效近似VPP的能量-调节可行域。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于更好地利用DERs的灵活性，为VPP作为整体参与市场出清和调度提供了可行的FR提交方案。

Abstract: System operators will probably allow virtual power plants (VPPs) to submit their feasible region (FR) for market clearing and dispatch. A VPP needs to determine its FR to submit as a whole based on the individual operation model of its internal distributed energy resources (DERs), which is an FR aggregation problem. Existing FR aggregation approaches rely on analytical methods, which have issues with generality and adaptability. In this paper, we propose a data-driven approach to approximate the energy-regulation FR of VPPs. It adopts the virtual battery model to approximate the aggregate FR of a VPP and determines the model parameters through inverse optimization based on generated multi-scenario operation data using the original operation model. Numerical tests verified the accuracy of the proposed method. We believe that our work helps to better leverage the flexibility of DERs.

</details>


### [5] [Schrödinger Bridges over Kinetic Swarming Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25281)
*Asmaa Eldesoukey,Md Zulfiqur Haider,Italo Napolitano,Yongxin Chen,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究受随机扰动影响的惯性集群在有限时间内的最小能量集体转向问题，以Cucker-Smale对齐和Morse吸引-排斥交互为系统动力学，利用Schrödinger桥理论将无控制的交互随机动力学视为先验模型，推导出具有时间对称结构的耦合最优性条件，并提出嵌套定点迭代数值求解方案。数值实验表明最优校正漂移能根据交互力对转向任务有利还是不利，动态地利用或对抗这些交互力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物启发的群体系统中，仅靠智能体间交互往往无法在指定时间范围内将群体驱动到期望的聚合构型，这在靶向治疗、集体运输、紧急疏散等应用中至关重要。现有研究缺乏针对惯性集群在随机扰动下、给定端点分布约束的最小能量集体转向控制方法。

Method: 基于Schrödinger桥理论，将无控制的交互随机动力学（Cucker-Smale对齐或Morse吸引-排斥）视为先验模型，推导状态反馈控制下的最小能量校正漂移。构建非线性耦合的必要最优性系统（具有时间对称结构），并设计嵌套定点迭代方案进行数值求解。端点约束可为全相空间分布（位置与速度）或仅位置边缘分布。

Result: 数值实验表明，所获得的最优控制（校正漂移）能够根据交互作用对转向任务的性质（有利或不利），动态地增强（利用）或抵消（对抗）系统的交互力，从而实现有限时间内的精确集体转向。

Conclusion: Schrödinger桥框架为随机惯性集群的有限时域最小能量集体转向提供了统一的数学形式化方法，该框架能够灵活处理不同的交互模型和端点分布规格，在实际应用中具有广泛潜力。

Abstract: Paradigmatic interaction models explain how collective behaviors can emerge in complex systems from interactions among the constituent agents. In bio-inspired swarms, however, interactions alone may not suffice to bring the population to a desired aggregate configuration within a prescribed time horizon, as needed in applications ranging from targeted therapy to collective transport and emergency evacuation. In the present work, we consider finite-horizon minimum-energy collective steering for inertial swarms that are subject to stochastic disturbances. We focus on the mean-field representations of these multi-agent systems driven by Cucker--Smale alignment or Morse attraction--repulsion interactions. Our objective is to steer the swarm between prescribed endpoint distributions using a state-feedback control, where the endpoint specifications can be full phase-space distributions (positions and velocities) or position marginals alone. Our formalism is rooted in the theory of Schrödinger bridges, which has inspired contemporary developments spanning statistical inference, biological modeling, stochastic control, and generative learning. Within the bridges framework, the uncontrolled interacting stochastic dynamics are viewed as a prior model, and the optimal control as the minimum-energy corrective drift needed to realize the prescribed distributions. We derive nonlinear, coupled necessary optimality systems with a time-symmetric structure reminiscent of classical Schrödinger bridges, and propose nested fixed-point schemes to numerically solve them. Numerical examples show that the obtained optimal control (corrective drift) can dynamically exploit or counteract the interaction forces, depending on whether the latter are favorable or adversarial to the steering task.

</details>


### [6] [Optimal Control in Hilbert Complex Spaces with Finite Element Exterior Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25266)
*Farid Bozorgnia,Michael Holst,Anshu Kumar,Vyacheslav Kungurtsev,Martin Licht,Gantumur Tsogtgerel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了在Hilbert复形上进行PDE约束最优控制的框架，并利用有限元外微积分（FEEC）进行结构保持离散化。框架将规范固定的局部状态与代表全局环流或通量的调和分量分离，引入有限维拓扑执行器，其可达模式由域拓扑和执行器秩共同决定。论文建立了适定性、弱最优条件、可达性结果和保持调和结构的FEEC误差估计，并通过数值实验及时间依赖Maxwell控制的推广验证了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在PDE约束最优控制中，当问题域具有非平凡拓扑时会产生物理上有意义的全局模式（如环流、通量），传统方法难以正确处理这些调和分量与局部状态之间的耦合，也缺乏对这类问题结构保持的离散化理论。

Method: 基于Hilbert复形建立PDE约束最优控制的统一框架；利用Hodge分解将规范固定的局部状态与调和分量分离；引入有限维拓扑执行器描述控制输入；采用FEEC进行结构保持离散化，确保调和结构在离散层面得到保留；建立了适定性、最优性条件、可达性定理及误差估计；将框架推广至时间依赖的Maxwell控制问题。

Result: 证明了问题的适定性与弱最优性条件；建立了由域拓扑和执行器秩共同决定的可达性结果；给出保持调和结构的FEEC误差估计；数值实验在单连通和多重连通域上展示了环流与腔通量控制、秩依赖的可达性、网格无关的优化行为以及拓扑诱导的奇异收敛；时间依赖Maxwell控制问题中调和电环流与磁通量成为具有显式守恒和可达性性质的动力学状态。

Conclusion: 该框架为具有非平凡拓扑的PDE约束最优控制提供了统一的理论与数值方法，调和分量的显式处理与FEEC结构保持离散化相结合，实现了对全局模式的精确控制，并可为更广泛的应用场景（如电磁场、流体环流控制）提供基础。

Abstract: We develop a framework for PDE-constrained optimal control on Hilbert complexes and its structure-preserving discretization by finite element exterior calculus (FEEC), with emphasis on problems where nontrivial topology creates physically meaningful global modes. We separate the gauge-fixed local state from harmonic components representing global circulation or flux, and introduce finite-dimensional topological actuators whose reachable modes are determined jointly by domain topology and actuator rank. We establish well-posedness, weak optimality conditions, reachability results, and FEEC error estimates preserving the harmonic structure. Numerical experiments on contractible and multiply connected domains demonstrate circulation and cavity- flux control, rank-dependent reachability, mesh-independent optimization, and topology-induced singular convergence. We further extend the framework to time-dependent Maxwell control, where harmonic electric circulation and magnetic flux become dynamical states exhibiting explicit conservation and reachability properties.

</details>


### [7] [Accelerated Gradient Flow with Endogenous Gradient-Memory Anchor: Selection and Restoring Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25312)
*Chinedu Izuchukwu,Ernest Obini,Jen-Chih Yao,Shengda Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带有内生梯度记忆锚点和非线性恢复-阻尼反馈的加速梯度流动力学。该动力学仅使用目标函数的一阶信息，无需显式Hessian信息。在适当假设下，建立了强解的全局存在性与唯一性，并通过Lyapunov分析获得F(x(t))-F* = O(t^{-2})的加速目标值估计。当解集S为仿射时，极限被显式识别为初始锚点z0到S上的欧几里得投影，对秩亏最小二乘问题可实现最小范数解选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的加速梯度方法（如Nesterov加速梯度）虽能获得O(t^{-2})的收敛率，但对收敛点的选择缺乏控制，尤其在非严格凸或秩亏问题中，不同初始点可能收敛到不同解。本文旨在设计一种仅依赖一阶信息的加速动力学，既能保持最优的收敛速率，又能通过锚点机制实现特定的极小元选择（如最小范数解），从而弥合加速优化与解选择之间的gap。

Method: 方法核心包含两个创新组件：(1) 内生梯度记忆锚点z(t)，由梯度历史加权演化而来，充当动态参考点；(2) 非线性恢复-阻尼反馈项，由当前状态与锚点的坐标平方位移调制。整个系统仅使用一阶梯度信息，不涉及Hessian。理论分析框架包括：利用标准ODE理论建立强解的存在唯一性；通过构造合适的Lyapunov函数导出O(t^{-2})的目标值衰减率；在解集仿射的假设下，利用动力学结构特征证明轨迹强收敛到初始锚点在解集上的欧几里得投影；对相位变量建立有限加权耗散估计；在特定区域分析了均匀相位动力学中非线性反馈带来的额外多项式衰减因子。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 强解的全局存在唯一性；(2) Lyapunov分析给出加速目标值估计F(x(t))-F* = O(t^{-2})，且非线性恢复-阻尼项提供额外耗散；(3) 原始轨迹强收敛到极小元；(4) 当解集S为仿射时，极限恰为欧几里得投影P_S(z0)，对秩亏最小二乘问题即选择最接近z0的最小二乘解；(5) 相位变量的有限加权耗散估计；(6) 在坐标分离条件下，均匀相位动力学获得额外多项式衰减因子。数值实验验证了极小元选择、加速目标值衰减和恢复-阻尼机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种新颖的加速梯度流框架，通过内生梯度记忆锚点和非线性恢复-阻尼反馈的统一设计，同时实现了最优收敛速率和可控的极小元选择能力。这为加速优化方法在非唯一解问题中的应用提供了理论保证，尤其是在秩亏最小二乘等实际问题中，能够自然选择最接近给定参考点的解。未来工作可探讨离散化格式及随机化拓展。

Abstract: We introduce an accelerated gradient flow with an endogenous gradient-memory anchor and a nonlinear restoring--damping feedback. The anchor evolves from a weighted history of the gradients, while the nonlinear feedback is modulated by the squared coordinatewise displacement from the anchor. The resulting dynamics uses only first-order information from the objective function and does not involve explicit Hessian information. Under suitable assumptions, we establish global existence and uniqueness of strong solutions. A Lyapunov analysis yields the accelerated objective-value estimate $F(x(t))-F^\star=\mathcal{O}(t^{-2})$, with the nonlinear restoring--damping term contributing additional dissipation. Under similar assumptions, the primal trajectory converges strongly to a minimizer. When, in addition, the solution set $S$ is affine, the limit is identified explicitly as the Euclidean projection $P_S(z_0)$ of the initial anchor $z_0$ onto $S$. In particular, for rank-deficient least-squares problems, the dynamics selects the least-squares solution closest to $z_0$. Numerical experiments illustrate the minimizer-selection, accelerated objective-value decay, and restoring--damping mechanisms.

Abstract: Accurate online traffic prediction is essential for intelligent transportation systems, where forecasting must be performed continuously under imperfect sensing conditions. Missing observations and anomalous disturbances make this task challenging, particularly when prediction relies on a single traffic view. A group sparse regularization is further introduced to capture correlated abnormal responses induced by real traffic anomalies and thus reduce their influence on forecasts. For streaming deployment, MVCTD performs iterative refinement only on the current latent tensor, while the remaining model variables are updated by lightweight closed-form steps based on summarized historical information, thereby avoiding repeated optimization over the full historical sequence. Abstract: This paper investigates inertial primal-dual dynamics with implicit Hessian-driven damping for strongly convex optimization problems with linear equality constraints. Using Lyapunov-based method, we show that the proposed algorithm achieves convergence rates consistent with those of its continuous-time counterpart. We also extend the obtained results to non-smooth convex optimization case. Furthermore, we conduct numerical experiments to illustrate theoretical results.

Abstract: Variable-metric proximal methods accelerate composite convex optimization,
  but the scaled proximal map induced by a quasi-Newton metric rarely
  has a closed form. We develop \emph{Decomposed Bilevel Search} (DBS), based
  on a diagonal-plus-rank-one factor \(X=D+uv^\top\) whose diagonal scaling
  satisfies a weak secant equation. The induced inverse metric
  \(B^{-1}=XX^\top\) recovers zero-memory DFP/BFGS-type Broyden members, while
  the factor form reduces each scaled proximal step to a two-dimensional
  monotone residual system. Each residual evaluation requires one
  diagonal-metric proximal map, and a certified bilevel solve reaches target
  accuracy \(ε\) in
  \(\mathcal O((d+T_p)\log^2(1/ε))\) work, where \(T_p\) is the cost of
  that proximal map. Under strong convexity, the outer method converges
  linearly with exact and inexact inner solves. In the scalar
  specialization \(D=αI\), the oracle uses only ordinary proximal
  evaluations of the regularizer and no generalized Jacobian or active-set
  information.

  logistic regression evaluate both the inner oracle and the full outer
  method. The oracle solves
  high-dimensional SLOPE scaled proximal subproblems of
  condition number up to \(4\times10^6\) using a few hundred ordinary
  proximal evaluations. In SLOPE outer benchmarks, the
  warm-started oracle keeps the scaled-proximal overhead controlled and DBS
  reaches stringent targets with far fewer gradient evaluations than
  Lipschitz-normalized FISTA; on
  \texttt{real-sim} this becomes a clear target-time advantage. On
  group-lasso logistic regression, DBS is reliable on synthetic correlated
  instances and fastest on a real-data grouped-copy instance.

Abstract: Berge's maximum theorem ensures the continuity of the value function and the upper semicontinuity of the solution mapping in parametric optimization problems. This theorem plays a central role in optimization theory, game theory, and dynamic programming. Motivated by the inherent inaccuracies in optimization data, this paper investigates the stability of such problems under sequential perturbations of both the objective function and the feasible mapping. The analysis focuses on the convergence of sequences of value functions and solution mappings via variational approximations of the data. To this end, we employ lower and upper continuous, epi- and hypo-convergence notions for functions, together with lower and upper continuous and graphical convergence notions for multifunctions. In addition, we study some relationships among these types of convergence and provide examples and counterexamples associated with the corresponding notions. Our results extend and complement existing stability results in the literature. We provide applications to generalized Nash equilibrium problems, where stability is obtained via a direct approach, as well as to finite-horizon dynamic programming models under novel perturbation assumptions.

Abstract: Classical Nesterov acceleration employs different choices of damping and inertial parameters in the convex and strongly convex settings, both for continuous-time dynamics and for discrete algorithms. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods when the strong convexity parameter is small.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有未知参数的优化应用中，传统方法完全依赖数据集来评估策略性能，但人类往往拥有无法从数据中获得的领域知识和物理世界交互信息，这些信息暗示名义问题的最优值不太可能很大。现有方法未能利用这种信念来提供更紧的性能保证，因此需要一种能够整合人类专业知识、在信念正确时给出更严格性能界的方法。

Method: 提出一种策略评估方法，将关于名义问题最优值的信念纳入性能保证的推导中。主要理论贡献是证明了：在策略最坏情况性能为一个凸规划的条件下，人类专业知识可带来的最大性能保证改进恰好等于某最大最小问题的极小极大间隙（minimax gap）。该方法为人类知识对算法性能边界的量化贡献提供了理论刻画。

Result: 理论结果表明人类专业知识的价值可精确地表示为极小极大间隙，并在品类优化和最短路径两个经典应用场景中对所提方法与理论结果进行了演示与验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用非数据驱动的定性信念改进优化策略的性能保证提供了理论框架，证明了在这种信念下可以获得更紧的保证，且人类专业知识的量化价值具有清晰的理论表达式，为实际应用中整合领域知识到优化决策提供了指导。

Abstract: We consider optimization applications with unknown parameters where the decision maker believes that the optimal value of the nominal problem-the optimization problem they would have solved if the true parameters were known-is unlikely to be large. This belief derives from information that humans have that is not captured in datasets, obtained from domain knowledge and interacting with the physical world. We propose an approach to evaluating policies that provides tighter performance guarantees if the decision maker's belief happens to be correct. Our main result shows that if computing a policy's worst-case performance is a convex program, then the value of human expertise-the maximum improvement in performance guarantees that can be obtained from the belief about the nominal problem-is equal to the minimax gap of a max-min problem. We illustrate our developments in assortment optimization and shortest path problems.

</details>

### [14] [Heat Transfer and Torque in Enclosing Cylindrical Configurations with Nonreciprocal Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24967)
*Dhruv Shah,Kiryl Asheichyk,David Gelbwaser-Klimovsky,Noah Graham,Mehran Kardar,Matthias Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究电磁涨落可同时传递能量和角动量，在包围（非平衡）构型中，若两物体中至少一个具有旋转对称性，则力矩受热传递约束。若两物体均旋转对称，须借助非互易介质打破n与-n的对称性才能产生非零力矩。针对同心圆柱体系，作者用Rytov涨落电动力学推导了热传递与力矩的角动量分辨通量密度公式，并通过邻近力近似分析了内柱稳定悬浮条件（得到稀薄板间Casimir力新解析式）。最后计算了慢速旋转内柱的涨落诱导摩擦，建立了力矩、摩擦与热传递之间的界，从而保证热机效率不超过Carnot极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁涨落引起的角动量转移使得在非平衡构型中可产生力、力矩、热流和摩擦。作者希望理解并约束这些效应之间的定量关系，特别是在包围构型（如同心圆柱）中探究力矩与热传递之间的内在联系，并为接触式热机能量提取（即非接触热机）的效率界限提供物理基础。

We then specialize to concentric cylinders with a nonreciprocal dielectric response and use Rytov fluctuational electrodynamics to express heat transfer and torque in terms of an angular-momentum-resolved flux density, $Φ_n(ω)$. We also analyze the conditions for stable levitation of the inner cylinder using the proximity force approximation, in the process obtaining a new analytic formula for the normal Casimir force between dilute plates at different temperatures. Abstract: The Johnson-Nyquist theory is commonly implemented by representing a conductor as a collection of independent local thermal-noise sources whose strength is determined by the local temperature. Abstract: The entropy production rate (EPR) quantifies irreversibility of a nonequilibrium steady state, yet standard formulas obscure how a complex interaction network generates it. Abstract: Relaxation toward equilibrium is usually accelerated by modifying the environment or by cooling a system further from equilibrium. Here we introduce a distinct strategy: thermalization packets, auxiliary systems prepared in advance and later coupled to a target system to accelerate its relaxation toward a prescribed thermal state. Thus, thermalization packets trade preparation effort for reduced waiting time. When the objective is cooling, we colloquially refer to such packets as ice cubes. Unlike ordinary coolants, thermalization packets are characterized not only by their temperature or heat capacity, but also by their microscopic preparation. We define perfect and optimal packets by their ability to suppress the slowest relaxation mode of the coupled dynamics: perfect packets eliminate it entirely, while optimal packets minimize its amplitude. We show that, under generic conditions, perfect packets exist among thermal preparations near equilibrium. Surprisingly, for asymptotic relaxation, the optimum among thermal preparations is generally not the coldest packet: cooling the packet beyond the slow-mode-cancelling optimum restores a nonzero slow mode and can therefore slow relaxation, yielding a packet analog of the Mpemba effect. We demonstrate the concept in exactly solvable Metropolis dynamics, a minimal two-qubit model, and a boundary-coupled interacting Ising-spin system. We also extend the framework to packets optimized for finite readout times. Finally, we show that the perfect-packet contour can connect the trivial bath-equilibrium point to a nontrivial strong Mpemba or strong inverse-Mpemba point.

Abstract: Work extraction from nonequilibrium systems is a major challenge across biological, chemical, physical, and engineering systems. Here, we consider protocols restricted to a finite number $N$ of intermediary Hamiltonians, consisting of a sequence of quench-relax steps. We determine the sequence of quenches that minimizes the dissipated work, which can be expressed in terms of a recurrence involving the Lambert function. We obtain lower bounds on work extraction from a nonequilibrium distribution as a function of its Fisher-Rao distance to equilibrium. We extend and apply the framework in two simple models: (i) an optical trap experiment, showing that the optimal intermediary distribution can be bimodal even for unimodal initial and final distributions, and (ii) an enzyme-catalyzed reaction, showing that that accounting for relaxation time in addition to dissipation can favor barrier-lowering.

Abstract: We investigate the statistics of the time-integrated current in an infinite one-dimensional gas of independent active Ornstein--Uhlenbeck particles, as a model system for studying an active generalisation of the corresponding passive (diffusive) phenomenology. Unlike the latter, where current fluctuations exhibit universal sub-diffusive scaling, active systems display diffusive, super-diffusive, and sub-diffusive regimes over different time scales. We fully characterise the distribution of current in terms of large-deviation asymptotics, showing that all of these scaling regimes are described by a single scaled cumulant generating function. Moreover, the statistics retain a dependence on the initial condition even at large times, revealing a persistent memory of the initial state. We further obtain the joint large-deviation statistics of currents measured at two distinct times, characterising temporal correlations. Our analytical predictions are verified using rare-event importance sampling, which resolves probabilities as small as $10^{-1000}$.

In 3D, we uncover new finite-time scaling relations for the active-inactive phase transition of the SSEP, and reveal a broadly consistent scaling exponent of the phase-transition point versus system size, implying that the phase-transition point is asymptotically controlled by the characteristic length scale ($s_c\sim L^{-2}$) regardless of dimension. TL;DR: 本文提出动态影响权重（DIW）方法，利用训练阶段可用的四路IMU数据提升仅使用右臂单一IMU的推理模型性能。DIW通过对折叠内部训练参与者进行一步候选更新测试，为logit损失和特征损失分配独立的逐样本门控权重。在WEAR数据集（22名受试者、19个标签、68,298个完整窗口）上，DIW的宏观F1达到0.638451，相比监督学习（0.561820）和固定权重知识蒸馏（0.571623）分别提升7.66和6.68个百分点，且在18/19个标签和21/22个受试者上优于监督学习。推理阶段仍保持同一80,915参数的右臂学生模型，不改动部署传感配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多位置惯性传感器可提升活动识别性能，但推理阶段要求全部传感器会增加部署负担。作者希望在训练阶段利用四个IMU的丰富信息，同时保持推理阶段仅用单个右臂IMU的低部署成本，解决固定权重知识蒸馏中教师目标对所有样本施加同等强度、而学生并非均等受益的问题。

Method: 提出动态影响权重（DIW）：在独立于正式训练样本的折叠内部参与者上测试一步候选更新，据此为每个训练样本的logit损失和特征损失分配独立的逐样本门控权重，从而动态调整各样本对两类蒸馏目标的贡献。师生框架中，冻结的四IMU教师提供logit与特征目标，右臂单IMU学生保持固定网络结构和参数量。

Result: 在WEAR数据集上，采用受试者不相交的五折交叉验证，DIW达到0.638451的池化折叠外宏观F1，相比监督学习（0.561820）和固定权重KD（0.571623）分别提升7.66和6.68个百分点；并在18/19个标签和21/22个受试者上超越监督学习。

Conclusion: 在该实验协议下，DIW能够将训练阶段的多位置信息有效转化为更强的单IMU模型，推理时无需改变部署传感配置或学生前向计算图，为多传感器知识向单传感器模型的迁移提供了有效且部署友好的方案。

Abstract: Inertial sensors at multiple body locations can improve activity recognition, but requiring every sensor at inference increases the deployment burden. We study whether four synchronized IMUs available during training can improve a student that uses only the right-arm IMU during fitting and inference. A frozen four-IMU teacher provides logit and feature targets. Fixed-weight knowledge distillation applies each target with the same strength to every fitting sample, although the student may not benefit equally from them. We introduce dynamic influence weighting (DIW), which tests a one-step candidate update on separate fold-internal training participants. DIW then assigns separate sample-wise gates to the logit and feature losses. On WEAR, we evaluate 19 labels and 68,298 complete windows from 22 participants using subject-disjoint five-fold cross-validation. Pooled out-of-fold macro-F1 is 0.561820 for Supervised and 0.571623 for Fixed-weight KD. DIW reaches 0.638451, gains of 7.66 and 6.68 percentage points, respectively. It exceeds Supervised for 18 of 19 labels and 21 of 22 held-out participants. All three routes retain the same 80,915-parameter right-arm student at inference. Under this protocol, DIW converts training-only multi-position information into a stronger single-IMU model without changing deployed sensing or the student forward graph.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用两阶段训练流水线：(1) 从更大的教师模型中蒸馏知识到紧凑的学生架构；(2) 使用硬负样本挖掘进行领域特定微调。同时构建了包含340万query-passage对的数据集，其中15万为跨多个司法管辖区的众包精选样本。推理架构支持BF16、INT8、二值等多种量化级别，以适配资源受限部署场景。

Result: GreenLeaf-Tiny在Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB)上取得75.11%的成绩，在MTEB(Law, v1)上取得64.38%的成绩，在1B参数以下的模型中展现出具有竞争力的表现，同时凭借多级量化支持实现了高效推理。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结合高质量数据（尤其是人工精选样本）的领域特定训练，能够在专业化领域应用中显著提升模型性能。即使参数规模仅0.6B，通过蒸馏和领域微调策略也能达到具有竞争力的检索效果，验证了在专用领域中以紧凑模型实现高性能的可行性。

Abstract: We present GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny, a 0.6B parameter embedding model for legal domain retrieval. GreenLeaf-Tiny achieves 75.11% on the Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB) and 64.38% on MTEB(Law, v1),demonstrating competitive performance among models under 1B parameters. Our approach combines a two-stage training pipeline that first distills knowledge from a larger teacher model into a compact student architecture, then applies domain-specific fine-tuning with hard negative mining; a carefully curated dataset of 3.4 million query-passage pairs, including 150,000 human-curated samples across diverse legal jurisdictions; and an efficient inference architecture supporting multiple quantization levels (BF16, INT8, binary) enabling deployment in resource-constrained environments. We provide detailed analysis of our training methodology, architectural choices, and comprehensive evaluation across legal retrieval tasks. Our results demonstrate that domain-specific training with high-quality data can improve performance for specialized domain applications

</details>


### [46] [NVExplain: Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Latent Trajectory Analysis and Structure-Preserving Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25080)
*Muyan Anna Li,Manikandan Ravikiran,Aditi Gautam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的时间序列预测可解释性框架，通过语义流（semantic flow）将每个预测视野归因到时间相关的历史滞后，构建滞后-视野归因矩阵，并利用结构保留扰动和稀疏局部代理模型生成人类可读的时间连贯解释。实验表明该方法在忠实度和稳定性上优于或匹敌现有基线，且计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型在高风险场景中广泛应用，但现有事后解释方法往往忽略时间依赖性，且无法提供特定预测视野（horizon）的解释，导致预测结果难以被理解和信任。

Method: 将预测建模为潜在轨迹，引入语义流量化模型内部表示中信息随时间演化的过程；通过聚合语义流构建滞后-视野归因矩阵；生成结构保留扰动并拟合稀疏局部代理模型，产生人类可读且时间连贯的解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，语义流变体在忠实度方面达到或超过标准事后基线，同时计算效率显著更高；稳定性分析表明解释具有鲁棒性，并识别出需谨慎解释的适用区间。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种高效、忠实且稳定的时间序列预测可解释性方案，能够为每个预测视野提供时间上可溯源的解释，同时指出了解释方法在特定条件下应谨慎应用的边界。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.

</details>


### [47] [Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24937)
*Xudong Mou,Zexin Wu,Chuan Luo,Shiru Chen,Xudong Liu,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从假设驱动视角系统综述多模态异常检测（MMAD），将现有工作划分为常态假设方法与异常假设方法两大范式，并探讨基础模型对MMAD的变革性影响及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMAD在工业检测、网络安全等安全关键应用中日益重要，但现有文献在不同领域和模态组合间呈现碎片化，且现有综述多按架构分类而非按异常的定义与分离方式分类，缺乏统一的系统性视角。

Method: 作者形式化MMAD问题，识别其核心挑战背后的五种内在特性，将已有工作组织为两大互补范式：常态假设方法（通过表示学习、跨模态对齐和知识增强建模规则性）与异常假设方法（通过粗粒度、结构和语义异常注入来锐化决策边界）；同时调研基础模型通过可扩展预训练、灵活的跨模态迁移和新兴推理能力对MMAD的重塑作用，并汇编跨领域代表性基准与评估协议。

Result: 提出了一个以异常定义和分离方式为核心的统一分类框架，覆盖两大范式下的多种具体方法，分析了基础模型带来的三方面变革（可扩展预训练、跨模态迁移、推理能力），并整理了跨领域的基准与评估协议。

Conclusion: 指出了面向鲁棒、自适应和可解释MMAD系统的开放问题与未来研究方向，为后续研究提供了以假设驱动为核心的组织化参考框架。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection (MMAD) detects rare abnormal events from heterogeneous data sources and is increasingly used in safety- and reliability-critical applications such as industrial inspection and cybersecurity. Yet the literature is fragmented across domains and modality combinations, and existing surveys usually group methods by architecture rather than by how abnormality is defined and separated in multi-modal settings. We survey MMAD from an assumption-driven perspective. We formalize the problem, identify five intrinsic characteristics underlying its core challenges, and organize prior work into two complementary paradigms. The first, normality-assumption methods, models regularity via representation learning, cross-modal alignment, and knowledge enhancement. The second, anomaly-assumption methods, sharpens decision boundaries through coarse-grained, structural, and semantic anomaly injection. We also investigate how foundation models are reshaping MMAD through scalable pretraining, flexible cross-modal transfer, and emerging reasoning capabilities. Finally, we compile representative benchmarks and evaluation protocols across domains and highlight open problems and future directions for robust, adaptive, and interpretable MMAD systems.

</details>


### [48] [GRAPE: Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation for Query-Efficient High-Dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25116)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Feng Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAPE是一个两阶段的高维黑盒优化框架，首先通过闭式采集函数锐化局部梯度后验，然后选择条件下降下期望降幅最大的更新方向，理论证明其梯度锐化阶段单调减小局部不确定性，进度感知方向随后验锐化收敛到最速下降。实验显示在黑盒对抗攻击中实现5.4倍加速，在LLM提示优化中最终平均遗憾比第二名方法低3.8个log单位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维黑盒函数优化中，局部贝叶斯优化虽然缓解了维度灾难，但现有方法过度关注下降概率而忽视下降幅度，导致步长过于保守、收益微小，浪费了查询资源在"几乎确定下降但改善甚微"的方向上。

Method: 提出GRAPE两阶段框架：第一阶段通过闭式采集函数锐化局部梯度后验，第二阶段在选择更新方向时最大化条件于下降事件的期望降幅。理论分析证明梯度锐化阶段单调最小化局部不确定性，且进度感知方向在后验锐化后收敛至真最速下降方向。

Result: 在黑盒对抗攻击任务中，GRAPE相对基线平均实现5.4倍加速；在LLM提示优化任务中，GRAPE的最终平均遗憾比第二名方法低3.8个log单位。

Conclusion: GRAPE通过同时考虑下降方向的不确定性以及期望下降幅度，显著提升了高维黑盒优化的查询效率，为局部贝叶斯优化的目标函数设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Optimizing expensive, high-dimensional black-box functions remains a central challenge in modern machine learning and scientific discovery. While local Bayesian optimization mitigates the curse of dimensionality, existing techniques often prioritize the probability of descent over the magnitude of progress. This leads to overly conservative steps that yield negligible improvement, wasting queries on directions that are nearly certain to descend but offer little decrease. We introduce Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation (GRAPE), a two-stage framework that first sharpens the local gradient posterior via a closed-form acquisition function, then selects update directions by maximizing the expected decrease conditional on descent. Theoretical analysis proves that this gradient refinement stage monotonically minimizes local uncertainty and that the progress-aware direction converges to true steepest descent as the posterior sharpens. Empirically, GRAPE demonstrates superior query efficiency across high-dimensional tasks: in black-box adversarial attacks, it achieves an average 5.4$\times$ speedup over baselines, and on large language model prompt optimization tasks, it outperforms the second best method by a reduction of 3.8 log-units in the final average regret.

</details>


### [49] [ExFold: Unified Expert Folding for Training-Free MoE Prefill-Decode Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24938)
*Juntong Wu,Yifei Liu,Junyi Chen,Siqi Fan,Chaoran Feng,Minghao Li,Liujie Zhang,Weihang Chen,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ExFold，一个统一的免训练专家折叠框架，通过将budget外的专家贡献投影到保留专家上，同时加速MoE模型的prefill和decode阶段，在vLLM中实现最高1.41x TTFT和2.45x TPOT加速，同时保持约99%的原始平均质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然通过稀疏专家激活控制了每token计算量，但低延迟推理仍面临挑战：prefill阶段由token级专家计算主导（计算受限），decode阶段由batch级激活专家集的内存流量主导（内存受限）。现有免训练加速方法只优化单一资源代理，要么只优化每个token执行的专家，要么只优化batch激活的专家，且要么丢弃被排除专家的贡献，要么仅隐式近似。缺乏一个统一方法同时针对两个阶段的不同瓶颈。

Method: ExFold将prefill和decode统一建模为一个带预算的输出近似问题：仅执行阶段特定的受限专家集，同时利用校准的标量投影矩阵将budget外专家的贡献折叠到保留专家上。具体而言：(1) 基于"许多专家输出方向对齐但幅度不同"的观察，在无标注数据上校准一个成对标量投影矩阵；(2) 推理时用该矩阵将排除专家的贡献折叠进保留专家；prefill加速为token级Top-K折叠，decode加速为batch级专家池折叠，两阶段仅在选择保留专家的方式上不同，排除贡献由同一折叠机制恢复。实现为vLLM中的即插即用插件，含轻量专家折叠CUDA内核。

Result: 在vLLM中实现ExFold作为即插即用插件，使用轻量级专家折叠CUDA内核，实现最高1.41x的TTFT（首token时间）和2.45x的TPOT（每token输出时间）加速，同时保留约99%的原始平均质量。

Conclusion: ExFold证明了prefill和decode可以统一为一个共享的专家折叠框架，两个阶段仅在选择保留专家的策略上不同，而排除专家的贡献可以通过校准的标量投影器统一恢复。这为MoE低延迟推理提供了一种统一且有效的免训练加速方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity for strong quality while keeping per-token compute bounded through sparse expert activation. Yet low-latency MoE serving is increasingly challenging, because it spans two inference phases with fundamentally different bottlenecks: prefill is dominated by token-wise expert computation, whereas decode is constrained by memory traffic from the batch-wise activated expert set. However, existing training-free acceleration methods optimize only a single resource proxy, either the experts each token executes or the experts a batch activates, and either discard the excluded experts' contribution or leave it only implicitly approximated. In this paper, we propose ExFold, a unified training-free expert-folding framework for jointly accelerating MoE prefill and decode. ExFold casts both prefill and decode as one budgeted output-approximation problem: execute only a phase-specific constrained expert set while projecting the contribution of budget-excluded experts onto retained experts using calibrated scalar projectors. Motivated by the observation that many expert outputs are directionally aligned but differ in magnitude, ExFold calibrates a pairwise scalar-projector matrix on unlabeled data and uses it at inference time to fold excluded expert contributions into retained experts. Under this view, prefill acceleration becomes token-level Top-K folding, and decode acceleration becomes batch-level expert-pool folding. The two phases differ only in how retained experts are selected, while excluded contributions are recovered by one shared folding mechanism. We implement ExFold as a plug-and-play plugin in vLLM, with a lightweight expert-folding CUDA kernel, delivering up to 1.41x TTFT and 2.45x TPOT speedups while retaining about 99% of the original average quality.

</details>


### [50] [Toward Machine Learning with the Unit as a Primitive: Learning from Unit-Linked Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25118)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"单元"(unit)作为学习任务语义层面上的显式原语，指代数据所指向的持久个体。监督学习被形式化为单元条件响应定律的家族；同质性是其特例。学习器从数据中习得(tokenizer, response-law)对，其中tokenizer生成上下文单元token，共享响应定律读取该token。当身份无法解析时，单元绑架(unit abduction)从事实证据构建同类型token。单行观测可能无法区分异质单元世界与同质合并世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习通过样本来形式化，但样本所指代的持久个体常常是隐式的。论文主张将个体(unit)作为任务语义层次的显式原语，以澄清数据生成过程中个体异质性带来的语义模糊性。

Method: 将监督学习作为主要形式化专门化。学习对象为(T_φ, R_θ)对：tokenizer将上下文转化为单元token，共享响应定律形式读取该token。同质性被定义为所有单元条件响应定律重合的特殊情形。提出单元绑架机制，用于从事实证据构建单元token以替代可信解析器提供的查找token。通过单行观测与可信同单元对的对比，刻画了可区分性边界。

Result: 形式化结果表明：无链接的单行观测无法区分异质单元世界与同质合并世界；而可信的同单元对能够分离一个受限的见证(witness)。学习器规格若省略单元信息则为单元不敏感(unit-insensitive)的，同质性仍是世界侧响应家族的性质而非学习器属性。

Conclusion: 单元应作为学习任务声明中的显式原语，tokenizer是学习器侧表示单元影响的机制。该框架为处理个体异质性、身份解析和跨观测关联提供了统一的语义基础。

Abstract: Machine learning is usually formalized through samples, while the persistent individual to which multiple observed or possible events refer often remains implicit. We propose the \emph{unit} as an explicit primitive at the level of task semantics. A learning task first declares a population of persistent referents and a sameness criterion; the realized value $u$ denotes the selected referent. Supervised learning is the main formal specialization. Its semantic object is a family of unit-conditioned response laws. Homogeneity is the special case in which those laws coincide; a sample-only conditional is silent as to whether the world is homogeneous or the observed law is only the marginal of a heterogeneous family. What is learned from data is a pair $(T_φ,R_θ)$: a tokenizer that produces a contextual unit token and one shared response-law form that reads it. The structured class takes that form to be a simple relation in the token; a linear predictor is the running instance. The token is the learner-side representation through which the task-side unit affects prediction, while a learner specification that omits unit information is unit-insensitive; homogeneity remains a property of the world-side response family. When identity is unresolved, the world-side law mixes unit-conditioned targets, while the learner composes its shared form with a token. A trusted resolver may fix the unit and supply a lookup token; otherwise \emph{unit abduction} forms a token of the same type from factual evidence. Unlinked single-row observations can fail to distinguish a heterogeneous unit world from a homogeneous pooled world; trusted same-unit pairs separate a restricted witness. The formal results concern this supervised specialization.

</details>


### [51] [When Does Frequency Decomposition Benefit Physics-Informed Neural Networks? A Preliminary Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24940)
*Shubham Rai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双分支频谱门控架构（DBSG-PINN），将低频和高频分量拆分到独立的子网络中，并通过自适应门进行连接，用于缓解物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的频谱偏置问题。在五个一维基准PDE上的部分控制消融实验表明：频率分解在频谱复杂的基准上效果显著（相对L2误差最高降低59.2%），但在较平滑的PDE上收益有限；门的优势与目标解的频谱丰富度成正比。研究仅使用单一训练种子，属于探索性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在逼近偏微分方程时存在频谱偏置问题——网络学习低频分量比高频分量更快。现有的傅里叶特征嵌入和正弦激活等技术通常被默认认为有益，但缺乏系统性验证哪些频谱区间实际受益。本文旨在通过频谱分解和门控路由来分离低频与高频分量，并审视其在不同频谱复杂度基准上的实际效果。

Method: 提出DBSG-PINN架构，包含低/高频两个独立子网络分支，由自适应门（adaptive gate）连接，实现频谱分解与频谱路由。在五个一维基准PDE上进行部分控制的消融实验，对比频率分解、频谱路由等变体的性能差异，检验不同频谱复杂度问题下各组件的贡献。

Result: 频率分解在频谱复杂的问题上效果显著，多模态波动问题的相对L2误差降低59.2%；但在较平滑的PDE上收益甚微。在1D Wave基准上，完整模型比简单的固定组合变体表现更差。门的收益随目标解频谱丰富度增大而增大，在多尺度基准上优势最大，在单尺度基准上最小甚至为负，表明门确实利用了频率结构。

Conclusion: 频谱分解和门控路由的收益高度依赖于目标问题的频谱复杂度，并非普适有效。该研究为探索性研究，基于单一训练种子，旨在提出疑问而非给出定论；需在更多种子和基准上验证该规律是否成立。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) often have high-frequency and multi-scale features that neural networks struggle to approximate. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build the governing equations directly into training, but suffer from spectral bias: they learn low-frequency components faster than high-frequency ones. Techniques such as Fourier feature embeddings and sinusoidal activations address this, but most studies assume they help across the board without checking which spectral regimes actually benefit. We introduce a dual-branch, spectrally-gated architecture (DBSG-PINN) that splits low- and high-frequency components into separate subnetworks joined by an adaptive gate, and use it to run a partially controlled ablation of frequency decomposition and spectral routing. We test this on five one-dimensional benchmark PDEs, ranging from smooth, single-scale problems to oscillatory, multi-scale ones. Frequency decomposition helps most on the spectrally complex benchmarks, cutting relative $L_2$ error by up to $59.2\%$ on a multimodal wave problem, but gives little benefit on smoother PDEs. On one benchmark (1D Wave), it performs substantially worse than a simpler fixed-combination variant. The gate's benefit scales with how spectrally rich the target solution is: the full model's advantage over the ablations is largest on multi-scale benchmarks and smallest (or negative) on single-scale ones, consistent with the gate exploiting frequency structure rather than acting as noise,though we do not directly visualize or quantify its spatial activations in this study. All results come from a single training seed across five 1D benchmarks, so we present this as an exploratory study meant to raise questions rather than answer them, and outline the additional seeds and benchmarks needed to test whether the pattern holds.

</details>


### [52] [FAMPWQ: Fisher Information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization for Effective LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24945)
*Gongwei Lee,Ji Liu,Juncheng Jia,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Fisher信息的自适应混合精度权重量化方法FAMPWQ，通过强化学习分配逐层位宽，在保持LLM性能的同时实现资源受限设备上的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多个领域表现卓越，但其庞大的资源需求阻碍了在资源受限设备上的部署。虽然量化是有效手段，但传统量化方法因均匀位宽或简单启发式敏感性评估而导致严重的性能退化，亟需更精细的自适应量化方案。

Method: FAMPWQ包含两个核心组件：(1) 提出基于Fisher信息的新颖系统模型度量指标，用于衡量各层对量化的敏感性；(2) 提出基于强化学习的位宽分配器，根据Fisher信息敏感性指标生成自适应的位宽分配策略。

Result: 在7个模型和5个基准上的大量实验表明，FAMPWQ在PPL（最多降低3.39）、准确率（最多提升6.87%）以及LLM-as-a-judge比较（最高76%胜率）方面均显著优于7种基线方法。

Conclusion: FAMPWQ通过Fisher信息驱动的自适应混合精度量化有效解决了传统均匀量化带来的性能损失问题，为LLM在商用GPU上的高效推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable achievements of Large Language Models (LLMs) in multiple domains, while the excessive resource requirements of LLMs hinder the deployment on resource-constrained devices. Although model quantization stands out as an effective approach, conventional quantization approaches typically incur severe performance degradation due to uniform bit-width or simple heuristic sensitivity evaluation. In this paper, we propose a novel Fisher information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization approach, i.e., FAMPWQ, which performs layer-adaptive weight quantization for effective LLM inference on commodity GPUs. First, we propose a system model with a novel Fisher information metric to measure the layer-wise sensitivity to quantization. Second, we propose a reinforcement learning-based bit-width allocator in FAMPWQ, which generates an adaptive bit-width allocation strategy based on the Fisher information sensitivity metric. Extensive experiments on 7 models and 5 benchmarks demonstrate that FAMPWQ significantly outperforms 7 baseline approaches in terms of PPL (up to 3.39 smaller), accuracy (up to 6.87% higher), and LLM-as-a-judge comparison (up to 76% win rate).

</details>


### [53] [Representing MAX functions using two-hidden-layer ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25221)
*Zhimao Wang,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过计算机辅助搜索，使用特定形式的线性组合（形如 max{两个坐标对的最大值之和} 的项），成功构造出 MAX_5 至 MAX_8 的双隐层ReLU神经网络精确表示，为该开放问题（MAX_N 是否可用常数层甚至两层ReLU网络精确表示）提供了新的支持性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画连续分段线性函数（如 MAX_N）所需的最小隐层层数是ReLU神经网络理论中的一个基本开放问题。目前已知的最好下界为2，而最好上界为对数阶（log N），其间的巨大差距促使研究者探索是否存在常数层（甚至2层）的双隐层ReLU表示。

Method: 采用计算机辅助搜索方法，考虑形如 max{Σ_{r=1}^{s} max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}), Σ_{r=1}^{s} max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})} 的有理线性组合。每个内部二元最大值可在第一隐层计算，外部最大值在第二隐层计算，因此任何这些项的有理线性组合都具有二隐层ReLU实现。通过搜索找到 MAX_5 至 MAX_8 的此类恒等式。

Result: 成功获得 MAX_5、MAX_6、MAX_7 和 MAX_8 的双隐层ReLU精确表示。这些表示与 [Ruess et al., 2026] 独立获得的结果不同但存在共性；其中 MAX_5 的表示补充验证了 [Bakaev et al., 2026] 的最新成果。

Conclusion: 该结果进一步推进了 MAX_N 双隐层ReLU表示的最新边界（从N=5扩展到N=8），支持了常数层（甚至两层）足以表示 MAX_N 的猜想，为最终解决该开放问题提供了更多证据，并指出了与独立工作在某些技术细节上存在差异，可能对后续研究有参考价值。

Abstract: We study exact representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N(x)=\max{x_1,\ldots,x_N}$ using two-hidden-layer ReLU neural networks. This problem has been studied in recent years in an attempt to characterize the exact number of hidden layers required to represent continuous piecewise linear functions. The best lower bound is 2, while the current upper bound is logarithmic in $N$. It remains completely open if the right answer is a constant number of hidden layers (possibly even 2!) or not. In fact, a recent breakthrough was the representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_5$ as a two-hidden-layer ReLU function obtained in [Bakaev et al., 2026], and the case of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ was stated as open for $N\geq 6$ in that paper.
  Using a careful computer assisted search, we obtain two-hidden-layer ReLU representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_5, \mathrm{MAX}_6, \mathrm{MAX}_7$, and $\mathrm{MAX}_8$. We obtain these by considering rational linear combinations of terms of the form $\max\{\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})\}$, where $a_r,b_r,c_r,d_r\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$. Each inner maximum of two coordinates can be computed in a first hidden layer, and the outer maximum of the two side-sums can be computed in a second hidden layer. Consequently, every finite linear combination of these terms has a two-hidden-layer ReLU realization. An identity for $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ in this form therefore gives an exact two-hidden-layer ReLU representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$.
  Very recently, two-hidden-layer representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ of the above form were obtained for all $N\leq 10$ in [Ruess et al., 2026]. Our representations are different and were developed independently. While our techniques share most of the high-level ideas presented in [Ruess et al., 2026], there are also some minor differences which may be of interest for future research on this problem.

</details>


### [54] [MacroAgent: Regularity-Aware Macro Legalization with LLM-Agent-Designed Contour Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24946)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Xufeng Yao,Yuxuan Zhao,Yuntao Lu,Peiyu Liao,Zuodong Zhang,Yibo Lin,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MacroAgent，一种利用大语言模型（LLM）发现正则性感知轮廓算法的四阶段宏单元合法化框架（聚类、轮廓生成、模板匹配、簇间优化），在TILOS和Chipyard基准上相比现有最佳方法实现2-8倍布局正则性提升、3%-5%布线线长减少，并通过端到端PPA评估验证了实际收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLSI设计中宏单元占据核心区域的大部分，其位置对最终QoR有显著影响，而宏合法化是决定宏位置的最终步骤。现有宏合法化方法存在鲁棒性不足、计算成本高、或忽略宏间正则性等问题，需要一种更鲁棒且有效的解决方案。

Method: MacroAgent采用四阶段框架：（1）聚类（clustering）将宏分组；（2）轮廓生成（contour generation）利用LLM发现多个有效的启发式正则性感知轮廓算法；（3）模板匹配（template matching）为每个簇匹配最优布局模板；（4）簇间优化（inter-cluster refinement）协调不同簇之间的布局。核心创新在于利用LLM自动化地发现和生成鲁棒的正则性感知轮廓启发式算法。

Result: 在TILOS和Chipyard基准上与SOTA宏合法化方法相比：布局正则性提升2-8倍，布线线长降低3%-5%，拥塞可比且鲁棒性显著更好、运行时间可接受。端到端评估（Cadence Innovus）显示：相比DREAMPlace宏合法化基线，布线线长降低2.9%、TNS改善68.3%；集成到Innovus宏布局流程后布线线长降低1.8%。

Conclusion: MacroAgent通过LLM驱动的正则性感知算法发现，成功解决了现有宏合法化方法在鲁棒性、计算成本和正则性保持方面的不足，实现了布局正则性、线长和时序性能的全面提升，验证了LLM在EDA自动化算法设计领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Macros constitute a large part of the core area in modern very large-scale integration (VLSI) designs. Moreover, macro positions have a significant impact on the final quality of result (QoR), and macro legalization is typically the final step in determining the macro positions. However, existing approaches related to macro legalization either lack robustness or incur substantial computational costs or neglect the regularity between macros. To address these limitations, we introduce MacroAgent. The novel framework is a four-stage approach: clustering, contour generation, template matching, and inter-cluster refinement. We propose leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to discover multiple, effective heuristic regularity-aware contour algorithms. This framework successfully generates robust and effective algorithmic solutions for macro legalization. Compared with state-of-the-art macro legalization works, experimental results on TILOS and Chipyard benchmarks demonstrate a 2 to 8 fold improvement in layout regularity, a 3% to 5% reduction in routed wirelength with comparable congestion after global routing, and significantly better robustness with an acceptable runtime. Furthermore, end-to-end evaluation through Cadence Innovus place-and-route confirms that the regularity improvements translate into tangible PPA gains, including 2.9% lower routed wirelength and 68.3% TNS improvement over the DREAMPlace macro legalization baseline; it also achieves 1.8% lower routed wirelength when integrated into the Innovus macro placement flow.

</details>


### [55] [CAT-GS: Balanced Multimodal Learning via Calibrated Gating and Fusion Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24947)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Sharjil Khan,Md. Samiul Alim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAT-GS，一个基于神经动力学的优化控制器，用于解决多模态神经网络端到端训练中的三个耦合失效模式：模态不平衡、不稳定门控和融合干扰。CAT-GS在不修改模型架构、融合模块或任务损失的情况下，通过温度缩放和EMA平滑的教师置信度校准、margin阈值门控策略、带梯度预算重归一化的梯度稳定化，以及仅在融合层应用PCGrad来抑制跨模态梯度冲突。在音频-视觉、三模态和跨域基准上的实验表明，CAT-GS在多模态准确率上优于或匹敌强基线，并获得更平滑的门控行为和更少的融合梯度冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经网络的端到端训练常表现出不稳定的神经动力学，具体表现为模态不平衡（某一分支主导梯度优化）、不稳定门控（噪声置信度导致模态选择混乱）和融合干扰（模态特定梯度在共享融合层冲突）三种耦合的失效模式，这些模式共同劣化学习效果。现有方法未能同时解决这三个相互关联的问题。

Method: CAT-GS包含四个核心组件：(1)通过温度缩放和EMA平滑对教师派生可靠性进行校准，稳定置信度信号；(2)采用margin阈值化策略在warm-up dropout、弱模态优先和弱偏置混合三种策略间切换；(3)通过封顶梯度预算重归一化在激进门控下稳定梯度幅度；(4)仅在共享融合瓶颈处应用PCGrad（Projecting Conflicting Gradients）以减少破坏性的跨模态干扰。

Result: 在CREMA-D、AV-MNIST、VGGSound（音频-视觉）、UR-FUNNY（三模态）、CG-MNIST（合成数据）、AVE和CMU-MOSI（跨域基准）等数据集上，CAT-GS在融合多模态准确率上优于或匹敌包括OGM-GE、G^2D和UMT在内的强不平衡感知基线，同时展现出更平滑的门控行为和更少的融合梯度冲突。

Conclusion: CAT-GS作为一种纯反向传播阶段的优化控制器，无需修改模型架构、融合模块或任务损失即可有效缓解多模态训练中的不稳定神经动力学问题，为智能计算应用中的多模态学习提供了一种通用且侵入性最小的解决方案。

Abstract: End-to-end training of multimodal neural networks often exhibits unstable neural dynamics characterized by three coupled failure modes that degrade learning: (i) modality imbalance, where one branch dominates gradient-based optimization; (ii) unstable gating, where noisy confidence cues induce erratic modality selection; and (iii) fusion interference, where modality-specific gradients conflict at the shared fusion layer. We propose CAT-GS (Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery), a neural dynamics-based optimization controller for intelligent computing applications. CAT-GS operates during backpropagation without modifying model architectures, fusion modules, or task losses. Through calibration of teacher-derived reliability via temperature scaling and EMA smoothing, CAT-GS stabilizes neural dynamics using a margin-thresholded policy to switch between warm-up dropout, weak-modality prioritization, and weak-biased blending, stabilizes gradient magnitudes under aggressive gating via capped gradient-budget renormalization, and applies fusion-only PCGrad to reduce destructive cross-modal interference at the primary shared bottleneck. We evaluate CAT-GS on audio--visual multimodal pattern recognition benchmarks (CREMA-D, AV-MNIST, and VGGSound), a tri-modal setting (UR-FUNNY), controlled synthetic data (CG-MNIST), and additional cross-domain benchmarks (AVE and CMU-MOSI). CAT-GS improves or matches fused multimodal accuracy against strong imbalance-aware baselines (including OGM-GE, G$^2$D, and UMT) across settings, and yields smoother gating behavior with fewer conflicting fusion gradients.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond Optimal Rates in Stochastic Optimization: Trajectory-Adaptive Stopping Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25551)
*Liviu Aolaritei,Lucas Lévy,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强凸随机优化中的SGD算法，构造了完全可观测、轨迹自适应的上置信序列(time-uniform confidence sequence)，用于最后一轮迭代到优化器的平方距离以及加权平均的子最优性。这些界限同时随时间成立，在最优1/t衰减率上仅比迭代对数因子略差，并能适应实际随机梯度，使SGD能在无需牺牲统计有效性的前提下尽快停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SGD分析在算法运行前设定固定确定性的时间地平线，但实际应用中停止决策是自适应的（通过观察轨迹）。这种不匹配产生根本性认证问题：固定时间保证在数据依赖的停止时间下通常不再有效，而从最坏情况界限推导的确定性地平线则过于保守。为此需要构造能在任意（数据依赖）停止时间下仍然有效的轨迹自适应置信界限。

Method: 将SGD的演化轨迹视为一个序贯实验，其中观测为优化误差提供证据。作者开发了新的递归置信序列技术，以及一个适用于条件均值变化、可预测范围可无界增长的自适应过程的通用时间均匀经验Bernstein不等式。基于此构造平方距离和加权子最优性的上置信序列，并进一步推广到小批量SGD，其中经验Bernstein界利用每批内的实际二阶矩结构。

Result: 构造的界限在时间上同时成立，最坏情况下达到最优1/t衰减率（仅含迭代对数因子），并适应实际随机梯度的实现。数值实验表明，所提出的停止规则相比自然确定性时间层可能少几个数量级的迭代次数。

Conclusion: 这项工作解决了SGD在自适应停止时间下统计有效性的根本认证问题，通过时间均匀置信序列技术使得基于观测轨迹的自适应停止既可行又高效，显著减少了不必要的计算迭代。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is typically analyzed at a deterministic horizon chosen before the algorithm is run, even though practical stopping decisions are made adaptively by inspecting the evolving trajectory. This mismatch creates a fundamental certification problem: fixed-time guarantees do not generally remain valid at data-dependent stopping times, while deterministic horizons derived from worst-case bounds can be highly conservative. We address this problem for strongly convex stochastic optimization by constructing fully observable, trajectory-adaptive upper confidence sequences for the squared distance of the last iterate to the optimizer and the suboptimality of a weighted average. These bounds hold simultaneously over time, attain the optimal $1/t$ decay rate up to iterated-logarithmic factors in the worst case, and adapt to the realized stochastic gradients, allowing SGD to stop as soon as a prescribed accuracy is certified without sacrificing statistical validity. Our approach treats the evolving SGD trajectory as a sequential experiment whose observations provide evidence about the unknown optimization error. To formalize this perspective, we develop new recursive confidence-sequence techniques and a general time-uniform empirical Bernstein inequality for adapted processes with time-varying conditional means and predictable ranges that may grow without bound. We further extend these confidence-sequence constructions to minibatch SGD, with the empirical Bernstein bounds exploiting the realized second-moment structure within each minibatch. Numerical experiments show that the resulting stopping rules can require several orders of magnitude fewer iterations than natural deterministic horizons.

</details>


### [57] [Demystifying Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24949)
*Donovan Clay,Saket Gollapudi,Sankar Harilal,Min Jang,Jacob Morrison,Sewoong Oh,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对RL后训练算法进行逐步拆解，在可控简化环境中隔离RL with Verifiable Rewards的各个机制，揭示了RL后训练的成功如何受基础模型的先验分布、奖励信号的粒度、提示分布的多样性以及模型规模的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RL后训练已被广泛用于增强LLM的推理、数学和编码能力，但许多研究者和实践者仍将经典RL的原理视为"黑盒"，缺乏对其底层机制的系统性理解。

Method: 在受控简化环境中隔离RL with Verifiable Rewards的各步骤机制，使用策略输出分布的熵作为透镜，比较预训练、SFT和RL后训练所学习的分布差异，并分别考察基础模型先验分布、奖励信号粒度、提示分布多样性和模型规模等因素的影响。

Result: 研究发现'虚假奖励'的影响取决于后训练所用的提示分布；RL后训练的成功取决于基础模型是否已经在期望行为上留有足够的概率质量，这与经典RL中的探索概念相关联。

Conclusion: 本文为NLP社区提供一个将RL纳入工具箱的入门资源，系统阐明各因素如何交互影响后训练效果，帮助研究者理解RL后训练的底层原理。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), enabling impressive reasoning, math, and coding capabilities. Yet for many researchers and practitioners, the principles behind classical RL remain a "black box". In this work, we deconstruct the RL post-training algorithm, investigating each step to clarify what is actually happening beneath the surface. By isolating the mechanics of RL with Verifiable Rewards in a controlled and simplified environment, we examine how RL outcomes are shaped by the base model's prior distribution, the granularity of the reward signal, the diversity of the prompt distribution, and model scale. We use the entropy of the policy's output distribution as a lens to compare the distributions learned through pretraining, SFT, and RL post-training, revealing how each stage shapes model certainty. Our investigation sheds light on how these choices interact to affect post-training success. For example, we show that the effect of so-called 'spurious rewards' depends on the prompt distribution used for post-training. We also provide insight into why the success of RL post-training depends on whether the base model already places sufficient probability mass on the desired behavior, linking it to the classical concept of exploration in RL. Ultimately, we provide this primer as a resource to those in the NLP community wishing to incorporate RL as a tool in their toolbox.

</details>
