Motivation: 参数化稀薄流模拟面临高计算成本，而现有的确定性回归方法（如条件到潜在空间的MLP）在处理多值或随机解族时存在局限。作者旨在构建一个生成式条件框架，既能高效压缩稳态稀薄流解，又能通过采样式条件传输支持多值/随机解族，同时保留物理约束的适配能力。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：(1) 神经场自解码器将离散速度空腔解和DSMC圆柱解压缩到共享坐标解码器；(2) 仅含训练的PCA主成分图支持条件流匹配(FM)和扩散模型，无需确定性条件到潜在骨干网络；(3) 结构化低秩适配器(adapter)在冻结上游管线的前提下修正选定的解码器输出。

Result: 空腔基准外推插值的动理学相对L1误差达10^-5量级；圆柱基准确各场面积加权RMSE为密度0.038、温度0.041、速度低于0.01。物理适配使空腔BGK诊断误差降低28.65%；圆柱解析壁面映射严格满足无穿透条件，与FM适配器联合将入口违例降至0.277、全局质量平衡比为0.963，且场误差变化可忽略。五种子对照实验表明生成管线在点精度上未超越确定性MLP。

Conclusion: 生成式条件传输在共享表示上被验证为有效的稳态替代方案，虽然当前单值稳态问题上点精度不及紧凑MLP，但该框架为多值或随机解族提供了自然扩展路径，兼具高保真压缩与物理约束适配能力。

Abstract: We present a conditional latent generative framework for parametric rarefied flows that separates neural-field representation, latent transport, and frozen physics adaptation. Neural-field auto-decoders compress discrete-velocity cavity solutions and direct simulation Monte Carlo cylinder solutions into shared coordinate decoders. Train-only principal-component charts support conditional flow matching (FM) and diffusion without a deterministic condition-to-latent backbone, and structured low-rank adapters correct selected decoder outputs while the upstream pipeline remains frozen. On two steady benchmarks, the frozen pipelines interpolate out-of-sample conditions with cavity kinetic relative $L_1$ errors at the $10^{-5}$ level and cylinder per-field area-weighted RMSEs of 0.038 (density), 0.041 (temperature), and below 0.01 (velocities). For the cavity, physics adaptation reduces the matched-grid Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook diagnostic by 28.65% while preserving field accuracy; for the cylinder, the analytic wall map enforces no-penetration exactly and, jointly with the learned FM adapter, reduces the inlet violation to 0.277 and the global mass-balance ratio to 0.963 of the frozen values with negligible field-error change. A five-seed controlled comparison with deterministic condition-to-chart multilayer perceptrons shows that, although the generative pipelines do not surpass the compact MLP in point accuracy on these single-valued steady problems, the results validate sampling-based conditional transport on the shared representation as an effective steady surrogate, with a natural route to multivalued or stochastic solution families.

</details>


### [156] [A Scalable OpenLB LAMMPS Framework for Fully Resolved Simulations of Hindered Settling of Arbitrary Non-Spherical Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26030)
*Varghese Babu,Adrian Kummerländer,Mathias J. Krause,Santosh Ansumali,Dilip Gersappe*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可扩展的流体-结构相互作用（FSI）框架，将OpenLB中的LBM（格子玻尔兹曼方法）与LAMMPS中的DEM（离散元方法）耦合，用于模拟任意形状刚体在流体中的运动。通过多球'clump'表示捕获颗粒接触，并通过体素化在流体网格上解析颗粒几何。模型经过单颗粒和多颗粒基准验证后，研究了多达10万个立方体的受阻沉降，发现立方体与球体在沉降行为上存在显著差异：立方体形成明显的配位壳层但缺乏面平行接触，而球体则表现出接触主导的聚集。速度波动中的相关长度随系统尺寸扩展，突出了颗粒形态在悬浮动力学中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非球形颗粒的受阻沉降因接触力学和水动力相互作用的复杂计算挑战，其理解程度远不如球形颗粒。作者旨在开发一个能够模拟任意形状刚体在流体中运动的大规模可扩展框架，并探究颗粒形态（特别是立方体与球体对比）对受阻沉降动力学的影响。

Method: 采用耦合框架：将OpenLB中的格子玻尔兹曼方法（LBM）与LAMMPS中的离散元方法（DEM）耦合。颗粒接触通过DEM中的多球'clump'表示捕获，颗粒几何通过体素化在流体网格上解析。模型先经过单颗粒和多颗粒基准验证，然后应用于包含多达100,000个立方体的受阻沉降模拟。

Result: 立方体与球体在沉降行为上表现出明显差异：立方体形成明显的配位壳层但缺乏面平行接触，而球体表现出接触主导的聚集。速度波动的相关长度即使在最大研究系统中仍随系统尺寸扩展，表明系统的有限尺寸效应尚未完全消除。

Conclusion: 颗粒形态在悬浮动力学中起关键作用，该框架为模拟大规模岩土工程和颗粒流问题提供了鲁棒且可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Hindered settling of non spherical particles remains significantly less understood than spherical particles due to the computational challenges in resolving the complex contact mechanics and hydrodynamic interactions. In this paper, we present a scaleable fluid structure interaction (FSI) framework coupling the open-source LBM software in OpenLB with the Discrete Element Method (DEM) implemented in LAMMPS to simulate arbitrary shaped rigid bodies in a fluid. Particle contacts are captured using a multi-sphere "clump" representation in DEM, while particle geometries are resolved on the fluid grid via voxelization. We validate our model against single and multi-particle benchmarks, and proceed to study the hindered setttling of cubes in systems containing upto 100,000 cubes. Our simulations show distinct differences between the settling of cubes and sphere, as cubes form pronounced coordination shells without face-parallel contact, in contrast to the contact-dominated clustering observed in spheres. Our results find that correlation length in velocity fluctuations scale with system size even for the largest system studied. These findings highlight the role of particle morphology in suspension dynamics and demonstrate a robust framework for simulating large-scale geotechnical and particulate flows.

</details>


### [157] [An SPH--mesh Coupling for Vesicle Dynamics in Shear Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25522)
*Kuiliang Wang,Xinwei Cai,Ting Ye,Xuejin Li,Xin Bian*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）与三角化膜网格耦合的新型计算框架，用于模拟悬浮于流体中的囊泡动力学。该框架通过界面追踪方法自然地实现膜的不渗透性，无需粒子反射或反弹边界等非物理约束，并集成了四种弯曲能量模型（最小模型、自发曲率SC模型、双层耦合BC模型和面积差弹性ADE模型）。框架经平衡形态和坦克履带运动的严格验证，与既有理论及数值研究高度吻合，并对剪切流中不同弯曲模型对囊泡倾角、旋转频率和形态的影响进行了系统研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 囊泡在流体中的复杂动力学行为对许多生物物理过程至关重要，但现有数值方法在耦合膜力学与流体动力学时要么依赖非物理的边界约束，要么缺乏对不同弯曲能量模型的灵活支持，难以准确且通用地模拟囊泡的形状依赖性动态行为。

Method: 采用SPH方法求解流体，以三角化膜网格表示囊泡膜，通过新型界面追踪技术天然实现膜的不渗透性；膜模型集成四种弯曲能量公式（最小模型、SC、BC、ADE），系统比较各模型在剪切流中囊泡的倾角、旋转频率与形态演化。

Result: 框架在平衡形态与坦克履带运动中与理论及数值结果高度一致。研究发现自发曲率对稳态取向影响可忽略，但在低缩减体积下通过深颈哑铃状形态显著改变旋转动力学；BC和ADE模型则诱导特征性的不对称和口形红细胞（stomatocyte）形态。

Conclusion: 所提出的SPH-网格耦合方法是一种准确且鲁棒的数值工具，适用于探索囊泡在流体流动中复杂且形状依赖的动力学行为，为生物物理领域的囊泡模拟提供了通用平台。

Abstract: We present a novel computational framework that couples smoothed particle hydrodynamics~(SPH) with a triangulated membrane mesh to simulate the dynamics of vesicles suspended in fluids. A novel interface-tracking approach enforces membrane impermeability naturally, without resorting to non-physical constraints such as particle reflection or bounce-back boundary conditions. The membrane model incorporates four distinct bending energy formulations, namely the minimal model, the spontaneous curvature (SC) model, the bilayer couple (BC) model, and the area difference elasticity (ADE) model, providing a versatile tool for diverse biophysical scenarios. The framework is rigorously validated against equilibrium shapes and tank-treading motion of a vesicle, demonstrating excellent agreement with previous theoretical and numerical studies. A systematic investigation into the effects of each bending model on the vesicle's inclination angle, revolution frequency, and morphology in shear flow reveals key physical insights. Notably, spontaneous curvature has a negligible effect on steady-state orientation but profoundly alters rotational dynamics at low reduced volumes through the emergence of dumbbell-like shapes with deep constrictions. In contrast, the BC and ADE models induce characteristic asymmetric and stomatocyte morphologies. Our results establish the proposed SPH--mesh coupling as an accurate and robust tool for exploring the complex, shape-dependent dynamics of vesicles in fluid flows.

</details>


### [158] [Vortex filament dynamics and vortex ring motion revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25827)
*Andrew D Gilbert*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了理想不可压缩流体中细长涡旋的经典运动问题，通过引入与涡旋表面几何紧密相关的坐标系，使涡量方程在作用-角形式下大幅简化。利用微分几何工具处理非正交、时变坐标系，在仅假设涡旋细长性的条件下建立了一般数学框架，并成功应用于具有任意轴向流动的细长涡环运动计算，结果与先前研究一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长涡旋运动是流体力学中的经典问题，自19世纪Kelvin、Helmholtz、Hicks和Dyson以来已有众多模型用于模拟一般形状的细长涡管运动，但现有方法在处理涡旋几何变形和一般运动时仍存在简化不够彻底或适用范围受限的问题。作者希望通过引入与涡旋表面几何紧密关联的坐标系，从根本上简化涡量方程，并建立可推广到任意运动和变形的一般性数学框架。

Method: 作者引入一个与细长涡旋中涡旋表面几何紧密相关的坐标系，该坐标系是非正交且时变的，流体元素在该坐标系中的运动具有作用-角形式，使涡量方程大幅简化。由于坐标系复杂，采用微分几何工具描述涡量和坐标系统的结构，建立一般数学框架。在仅假设涡旋细长性（以管半径与曲率半径之比为小参数）的前提下推导方程组，允许涡核和曲线C(t)的任意运动与变形，并将该框架应用于计算具有任意轴向流动的细长涡环运动。

Result: 该一般框架成功应用于计算具有任意轴向流动的细长涡环的运动，直接求解了扰动涡旋表面的形状，所得结果与早期研究一致，验证了框架的正确性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的一般数学框架适用于未来开发简化的涡旋运动和相互作用方程，为细长涡旋的进一步研究提供了新的理论工具和推广空间。

Abstract: The motion of a slender vortex in ideal incompressible fluid is a classic problem in hydrodynamics. Formulae for the velocity of a vortex ring go back to work of Kelvin, Helmholtz, Hicks and Dyson in the nineteenth century, while more recently a number of models have been created for simulating the motion of slender tubes of vorticity of general shape. For a vortex tube or filament localised near to a curve C(t), the relevant small parameter to measure slenderness is the tube radius divided by the radius of curvature of C. The present paper revisits this range of classic problems by introducing a coordinate system closely linked to the geometry of vortex surfaces in a slender vortex. The motion of fluid elements in this coordinate system has an action--angle form, and with this the vorticity equation simplifies radically. At the same time, however, most aspects of the shape or evolution of a vortex are thrown into the description of the coordinates, and in particular the corresponding metric and volume form.
  As the coordinate system is non-orthogonal and time-dependent, tools of differential geometry are most easily used to describe the structure of both vorticity and coordinate system, and a general mathematical framework is set out. This resulting system of equations is taken as far as possible with only the assumption of vortex slenderness in place, but allowing arbitrary motions and distortions of the vortex core and of the curve C(t). The modelling is applied to calculate the motion of a slender vortex ring with arbitrary axial flow, solving directly for the shape of perturbed vorticity surfaces and giving results in agreement with earlier studies. The general framework set up in this paper is suitable for the development of simplified equations for vortex motion and interaction in future studies.

</details>


### [159] [Fock-Space Representation of the Lebowitz--Frisch--Helfand Kinetic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25833)
*Ilya Karlin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文构建了Lebowitz–Frisch–Helfand动力学方程的精确Fock空间表示。通过平方根Maxwellian变换将局部Ornstein–Uhlenbeck松弛扇区映射为玻色子数算符，并利用外部时空导数上的微分联络定义了任意容许实现映射下的拉回。作者证明了首阶传播算符的缠绕定理，物理速度矩由对偶Fock空间泛函表示，并通过传输–矩缠绕定理在不显式使用微分联络的情况下评估完整的传播贡献。在流体动力学极限下，数分级选择第二和第三Fock能级对应黏性应力和热流，直接导出Navier–Stokes–Fourier本构项。最后证明了局部坐标实现的协变性，并说明Hermite实现计算上具有特权，但缠绕Fock动力学、物理矩与兼容性结构在容许相似类内与表示无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学理论中，Fock空间/二次量子化方法大多局限于局部平衡或常数宏观参数的简单情形。当速度实现依赖于局部宏观参数（如温度、密度、流速）时，如何将完整的动力学方程（而非仅松弛项）精确地提升到Fock空间表示，是尚未解决的核心理论问题。作者旨在建立一套严格且表示无关的框架，涵盖任意容许实现映射、物理矩的Fock表示以及流体力学极限下的本构关系。

Method: (1) 以平方根Maxwellian变换为出发点，将局部OU松弛算符映射为玻色子数算符，暴露Fock空间的数分级结构；(2) 通过任意容许实现映射定义拉回，并证明外部时空导数在拉回下获得微分联络（连接项）；(3) 证明首阶传播算符的缠绕定理；(4) 用对偶Fock空间泛函表示物理速度矩，建立传输–矩缠绕定理，从而无需显式计算微分联络即可评估传播贡献；(5) 引入相容性条件（可用代数方式或动力学平衡律传播）；(6) 在流体动力学极限下利用数分级识别黏性应力与热流对应的Fock能级，导出NSF本构项；(7) 证明局部坐标实现变换下的协变性并给出Hermite多项式/Hermite函数坐标的显式说明。

Result: 获得了完整动力学方程的精确Fock空间表示：松弛部分为玻色子数算符，传播部分由缠绕算子与微分联络刻画，物理矩由对偶泛函表示并可通过传输–矩缠绕定理直接计算。在流体力学极限下，数分级自动选择第二、第三Fock能级，直接得到Navier–Stokes–Fourier本构项。所有结构（缠绕动力学、物理矩、相容性）在容许实现相似类内表示不变，且Hermite实现具有计算上的特权地位。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种精确、表示无关的Fock空间框架来描述完整动力学方程，弥合了此前仅能处理松弛扇区与完整动力学之间的鸿沟。微分联络与缠绕定理的组合使得在不显式求解联络的情况下评估传播贡献成为可能，而数分级在流体动力学极限下自然涌现出Navier–Stokes–Fourier本构关系。这为玻尔兹曼–类方程的高阶矩方法、谱方法及数值模拟提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop an exact Fock-space representation of the Lebowitz--Frisch--Helfand kinetic equation. The familiar square-root Maxwellian transformation is used only as a convenient starting point: it maps the local Ornstein--Uhlenbeck relaxation sector to the bosonic number operator and thereby exposes an elementary grading. The main issue is the representation of the complete kinetic dynamics when the velocity realization depends on local macroscopic parameters. We formulate the pull-back through an arbitrary admissible realization map, show that external space--time derivatives acquire a differential connection, and prove an intertwining theorem for first-order propagation operators. The physical velocity moments are represented by dual Fock-space functionals; a transport--moment intertwining theorem then evaluates the complete propagation contribution without requiring the explicit differential connection. Compatibility between realization parameters and kinetic moments may be imposed algebraically or propagated dynamically by exact balance laws. In the hydrodynamic limit, the number grading selects the second and third Fock levels responsible for viscous stress and heat flux, leading directly to the Navier--Stokes--Fourier constitutive terms. Finally, we prove covariance under local changes of coordinate realization and illustrate it explicitly for Hermite-polynomial and Hermite-function coordinates. Thus the Hermite realization is computationally privileged, while the intertwined Fock dynamics, physical moments, and compatibility structure are representation-independent within the admissible similarity class.

</details>


### [160] [A Compensated Koopman Neural Operator with Selective State-Space Dynamics for Unsteady Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25879)
*Tangying Lv,Yuanjun Dai,Zhenxu Sun*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出CoKo-UNO，一种结合U型谱骨干网络与Koopman主导隐空间传播的神经算子。通过有限维Koopman截断产生状态相关残差，在自回归滚动中反复注入，并利用选择性状态空间模型（SSM）对该残差建模，配合分辨率自适应补偿跳跃连接和重叠预热滚动策略。在四个基准问题上取得最低平均滚动误差，相比最强基线最高降低76.76%误差，且训练时间仅为RNO的约41.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非定常流动的稳定预测需要同时具备精确的多尺度空间表征和稳健的时间传播能力。现有方法在长时间自回归滚动预测中容易累积误差导致预测发散，因此需要显式的残差补偿机制来提升稳定性和精度。

Method: CoKo-UNO由三部分组成：(1) U型谱骨干网络负责多尺度空间特征提取；(2) Koopman主导的隐空间传播提供时间演化框架，有限维Koopman截断会产生状态相关残差；(3) 该残差通过选择性状态空间模型（SSM）建模，作为一种输入相关的补偿机制，在自回归滚动过程中反复注入以校正传播误差。此外还包含分辨率自适应补偿跳跃连接和重叠预热滚动策略提升训练的稳定性和泛化性。

Result: 在四个基准问题上，CoKo-UNO在所有对比方法中取得了最低的平均滚动误差，相对最强基线最大的误差降低达76.76%，同时训练时间仅为RNO的约41.40%，展现出色的精度-效率平衡。

Conclusion: 显式的残差补偿机制能够显著提升非定常流动的自回归稳定预测性能，证实了将Koopman理论与残差建模相结合的有效性，为长时间流体动力学预测提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Stable prediction of unsteady flows requires accurate multiscale spatial representation and robust temporal propagation. We introduce the Compensated Koopman U-shaped Neural Operator (CoKo-UNO), which combines a U-shaped spectral backbone with Koopman-dominated latent propagation. Finite-dimensional Koopman truncation produces a state-dependent residual that is repeatedly reinjected during autoregressive rollout. CoKo-UNO models this residual with a selective state-space model (SSM), a principled input-dependent compensation mechanism, together with resolution-adaptive compensatory skip connections and an overlapping-warmup rollout strategy. \NEW{Across four benchmark problems, CoKo-UNO achieves the lowest mean rollout error among all compared methods. Its largest gain is a $76.76\%$ reduction relative to the strongest baseline, while requiring about $41.40\%$ of RNO's training time.} These results show that explicit residual compensation improves stable autoregressive prediction of unsteady flows.

</details>


### [161] [Neural-Network and Reduced-order Modeling Workflows for AI-Driven CFD: Fast Response Surfaces, Reduced Dynamics and Jet in Cross-flow Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26064)
*Kaku E. Eduku,Pavel P. Popov,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本章提出一种AI驱动的CFD工作流，结合标量响应建模（MLP）与降阶动力学（POD-SINDy），以射流横流（jet-in-cross-flow）为例进行验证。MLP用于预测喷射器间距与未燃氢吞吐量、壁面传热和体平均温度浓度之间的映射关系；POD-SINDy用于场级雷诺应力统计的降阶建模。结果表明MLP在快速标量响应上有效，但传热与未燃氢吞吐量预测难度较大；POD-SINDy提供紧凑的场级预测，但总体误差比POD基重建高6.7%。结论是AI驱动CFD并非单一模型处方，需根据问题类型选择合适的建模策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率CFD仿真对设计至关重要，但代价高昂，无法支持密集的设计空间采样。作者希望提出一种兼顾精度与效率的AI驱动CFD工作流，在不牺牲物理信息的前提下实现快速设计空间探索。

Method: 采用两步策略：(1) 用多层感知机（MLP）将喷射器间距映射到未燃氢吞吐量、壁面传热和体平均温度浓度等标量响应，以形状保持插值为基线并采用留一样本交叉验证；(2) 用稀疏辨识非线性动力学（SINDy）作为降阶建模框架，对场导出的雷诺应力统计进行参数化场级预测。

Result: MLP成功识别出间距约八个射流直径处的中间到宽间距有利区域（非单调响应），其中体平均温度浓度预测稳健，而传热与未燃氢吞吐量预测难度显著增大。参数化SINDy模型在模拟及样本外间距下提供紧凑的场级预测，但总体间距平均雷诺应力误差比POD基重建高6.7%，源于部分工况下的退化。

Conclusion: AI驱动CFD并非单一模型的普适方案：MLP适合快速标量响应的预测，而POD-SINDy更适合以瞬态降阶动力学和场导统计为核心的应用场景。应根据问题特性选择相应的建模工具。

Abstract: Highly resolved computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations are essential for design but too expensive for dense design-space sampling. This chapter presents an AI-driven CFD workflow that combines scalar-response modeling and reduced-order dynamics using jet-in-cross-flow examples. A reacting hydrogen jet-in-cross-flow study is first used to train a multilayer perceptron (MLP) mapping injector spacing to unburnt hydrogen throughput, wall heat transfer, and bulk temperature concentration, with shape-preserving interpolation as a baseline. The CFD samples show a non-monotonic spacing response, and the MLP identifies an intermediate-to-wide favorable region near eight jet diameters. Leave-one-sample-out validation shows strong dependence on the predicted quantity: the bulk temperature concentration is robust, while heat transfer and unburnt hydrogen throughput are substantially harder to predict. Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) is then used as a reduced-order modeling framework for field-derived Reynolds-stress statistics. The parametric SINDy model provides compact field-level predictions at simulated and out-of-sample spacings, though its aggregate spacing-mean Reynolds-stress error is $6.7\%$ higher than the POD-basis reconstruction because of degradation at selected cases. The broader conclusion is that AI-driven CFD is not a single-model prescription: MLPs are effective for fast scalar responses, while POD--SINDy is better suited when transient reduced dynamics and field-derived statistics are central to the question.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Genus 4 Supermoduli Space Is Not Projected](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24996)
*Ron Donagi,Simone Noja*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了亏格4偶数自旋分量的超模堆（supermoduli stack）${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$是非投影的（non-projected），拓展了已知的亏格$g\ge 5$时非投影的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知光滑无奇点的亏格$g$超黎曼曲面超模堆${\mathfrak M}_g$在$g\ge 5$时是非投影的。作者希望将这一结论推广到更低的亏格情形，具体针对亏格4的偶数自旋分量，从而更深入地理解超模堆的投影性质。

Method: 作者在自旋模空间${\mathcal S\mathcal M}_4^{+}$中构造了一条适当的紧曲线，然后计算阻碍${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$投影性的特征类在该曲线上的限制。通过证明该限制非零，从而得出${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$是非投影的结论。

Result: 在自旋模空间${\mathcal S\mathcal M}_4^{+}$中选定的紧曲线上，阻碍投影性的类限制非零，由此证明了亏格4偶数自旋分量${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$是非投影的超模堆。

Conclusion: 亏格4的偶数自旋超模堆${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$是非投影的，这补充并扩展了超模堆非投影性的已知结果（此前仅知$g\ge 5$情形）。

Abstract: The supermoduli stack ${\mathfrak M}_g$, parametrizing smooth unpunctured super Riemann surfaces of genus $g$, is known to be non-projected for $g \ge 5$. We extend this result to the even-spin component of ${\mathfrak M}_4$. This is done by exhibiting an appropriate compact curve in the spin moduli space ${\mathcal S\mathcal M}_4^{+}$, and computing the restriction to it of the class obstructing the projectedness of ${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$. This restriction turns out to be non-zero, proving that ${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$ is non-projected.

</details>


### [163] [Kochen-Specker Configurations from Grids on Dual Quadrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25008)
*Giuseppe Favacchio*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架，用于构造和组织四维实数空间中的Kochen-Specker构型。该构造基于由欧几里得对极关联的两类光滑二次曲面上的有限网格，其关联几何直接产生正交测量上下文和量子语境性的奇偶性证明，生成无穷族构型，并为已知的循环构造提供了几何解释与推广。一个特殊子族可通过割线完成典范完备化，其前两个实例分别恢复F4和H4型例外根系构型，最小情形还恢复了Cabello构型及其在Peres构型中的嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kochen-Specker构型是量子语境性的核心证据，但多种著名的四维上下文构型（如F4、H4、Cabello和Peres构型）此前分别由不同的构造方法获得。作者希望建立一个统一的射影几何机制，用单一框架解释并生成这些看似无关的构型。

Method: 作者建立了一个几何框架：在四维实数空间中，选取由欧几里得对极（Euclidean polarity）关联的一对光滑二次曲面（quadrics），在其上构造有限网格。通过网格的关联几何直接推导出正交测量上下文，并利用奇偶性论证给出量子语境性证明。对特定子族，利用割线（secant lines）实现典范完备化，系统地生成构型族。

Result: 获得了无穷族的Kochen-Specker构型；对已知的循环构造给出了几何解释与推广；典范完备化方法的前两个实例分别恢复了F4和H4型例外根系构型，最小情形恢复了Cabello构型及其在Peres构型中的嵌入。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，多个此前通过不同方法获得的著名四维上下文构型（F4、H4、Cabello和Peres）实际上源于同一个射影几何机制，从而在量子语境性与经典射影几何之间建立了深刻而统一的联系。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for constructing and organizing Kochen--Specker configurations in real four-dimensional space. The construction uses finite grids on pairs of smooth quadrics related by Euclidean polarity. Their incidence geometry directly produces orthogonal measurement contexts and parity proofs of quantum contextuality, yielding infinite families of configurations and a geometric interpretation and extension of a previously known cyclic construction. For a distinguished subfamily, the same geometry admits a canonical completion determined by secant lines. The first two instances of this completion recover the exceptional root configurations of types $F_4$ and $H_4$, while the smallest case also recovers the Cabello configuration and its embedding in the Peres configuration. Thus several prominent four-dimensional contextual configurations, previously obtained from different constructions, arise from a single projective-geometric mechanism.

</details>


### [164] [Log MMP Constraints on Curves with Cuspidal Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25031)
*Jenia Tevelev*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用对数极小模型纲领，证明了Weimin Chen关于光滑射影曲面上具有单个Puiseux对的单尖有理曲线自交上界的猜想。该结果随后被推广到具有任意尖点奇点的有理曲线，并与Golla-Starkston的辛界进行了比较。最后，证明了不存在度为102、亏格为10、带有一个(36,289)-尖点的平面曲线，回答了Evans关于与McDuff-Schlenk楼梯最终后Fibonacci步相关的代数曲线的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weimin Chen在接触结构及其辛填充研究中提出了一个关于曲面上单尖有理曲线自交数的尖锐上界猜想，该猜想与接触几何、辛填充以及代数几何中的曲线奇点理论密切相关，需要利用对数极小模型纲领来证明。

Method: 采用对数极小模型程序(log MMP)作为核心工具，首先处理具有单个Puiseux对(p,q)的单尖有理曲线情形，证明自交上界猜想；然后将论证推广到具有任意尖点奇点的有理曲线情形；最后通过结合代数上与辛上的界来排除特定的平面曲线存在性。

Result: 论文证明了Chen关于单Puiseux对单尖有理曲线自交上界的猜想；将结果推广到任意尖点奇点的有理曲线，并给出与Golla-Starkston辛界的代数界比较；证明了不存在度为102、亏格为10、具有(36,289)-尖点的平面曲线，从而回答了Evans提出的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作通过对数极小模型程序建立了代数几何中曲线奇点自交界与辛几何中嵌入球面填充界之间的深刻联系，不仅解决了Chen的猜想和Evans的具体问题，还揭示了两大领域之间在低维拓扑与代数几何交叉处的深刻结构对应关系。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth projective surface and let $C\subset X$ be a unicuspidal rational curve with one Puiseux pair $(p,q)$. We use the log minimal model program to prove a sharp upper bound on the self-intersection of $C$ conjectured by Weimin Chen in [C, Conjecture 1.15] in the course of his study of contact structures and their symplectic fillings. We then extend this argument to rational curves with arbitrary cuspidal singularities and compare the resulting algebraic bounds with the symplectic bounds of Golla and Starkston [GS]. Finally, we prove that there is no plane curve of degree $102$ and genus $10$ with a $(36,289)$-cusp, answering a question of Evans [E, Remark 7.3.5] about algebraic curves associated with the final post-Fibonacci step of the McDuff-Schlenk staircase.

</details>


### [165] [From Spectral Curves to Variational Equations: Geometry and Differential Galois Theory of KdV Cnoidal Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25227)
*Juan Jose Morales-Ruiz,Jean Pierre Ramis,Maria-Angeles Zurro*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了KdV变分方程在椭圆余弦波（cnoidal waves）附近的谱几何描述，将其表示为两条相互作用的谱曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过谱几何这一现代数学工具，对KdV方程在cnoidal波解附近的变分方程给出清晰的几何结构刻画。

Method: 利用两条相互作用的谱曲线来描述KdV变分方程在cnoidal波附近的谱几何性质。

Result: 建立了KdV变分方程在cnoidal波附近的谱几何描述，以两条相互作用谱曲线的形式呈现。

Conclusion: KdV变分方程在cnoidal波附近的整体结构可由两条相互作用的谱曲线完整描述，提供了该问题新的几何视角。

Abstract: We provide a spectral geometric description of the KdV variational equation around cnoidal waves in terms of two interacting spectral curves.

</details>


### [166] [Dimensions of type $A$ Hessenberg varieties over a fixed sheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25205)
*Megumi Harada,Martha Precup,Colleen Robichaux*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在类型A旗簇中，固定Hessenberg函数h后，来自Lie代数gl_n(C)同一sheet的线性算子X所定义的Hessenberg簇都具有相同维数。这一结果将Goldin与第二作者关于最小sheet（minimal sheet）上的维度结果推广到了任意sheet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Goldin与第二作者已证明最小sheet上的Hessenberg簇存在到幂零Hessenberg簇的平坦退化，从而得到最小sheet上所有Hessenberg簇同维数。本文的动机是将这一维数结果推广到gl_n(C)中任意sheet，以建立更一般的几何不变量。

Method: 本文采用代数几何与表示论的方法，对Lie代数gl_n(C)中sheet的结构进行分析，利用Hessenberg簇的退化性质和sheet的分类理论，将最小sheet情形下的论证框架推广到一般sheet。

Result: 证明了对于固定Hessenberg函数h:[n]→[n]，由同一sheet中的线性算子X定义的Hessenberg簇Hess(X,h)具有相同的维数。

Conclusion: 该结果将Hessenberg簇维数不变性从最小sheet推广到任意sheet，深化了对Hessenberg簇几何结构的理解，并为Hessenberg簇的进一步研究提供了更广泛的理论基础。

Abstract: Hessenberg varieties $\mathcal{H}\mathrm{ess}(\mathsf{X},\mathbf{h})$ are subvarieties of the flag variety parameterized by a Hessenberg function $\mathbf{h}: [n] \to [n]$ and a matrix $\mathsf{X} \in \mathfrak{gl}_n(\mathbb{C})$. In recent work, Goldin and the second author showed the existence of flat degenerations of Hessenberg varieties to nilpotent Hessenberg varieties over the minimal sheet. This implies that all Hessenberg varieties over the minimal sheet have the same dimension. Our main result generalizes this dimension result to arbitrary sheets. Specifically, we prove that for a fixed Hessenberg function $\mathbf{h}:[n] \to [n]$, all Hessenberg varieties $\mathcal{H}\mathrm{ess}(\mathsf{X},\mathbf{h})$ defined in the type $A$ flag variety by linear operators $\mathsf{X}$ from the same sheet of the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{gl}_n(\mathbb{C})$ have the same dimension.

</details>


### [167] [Spin structures and measures on the moduli space of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25237)
*Paul Norbury*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文基于CIME暑期学校课程笔记，在光滑自旋曲线模空间上建立微分几何构造，重点在双曲几何中的解释，并区分研究偶与奇自旋分量，发现其体积贡献间的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将自旋曲线模空间的几何构造（特别是双曲几何解释）系统化，并扩展已有的局部常值层与全纯层的对应关系到具有Ramond标记点的自旋曲线，同时为模空间体积和体积递推提供Teichmüller理论框架，并揭示偶/奇自旋分量的对称结构。

Method: 利用双曲几何构造特征形式和自旋测度，建立双曲自旋结构对应的局部常值层与全纯层的联系，并分别分析偶和奇自旋模空间分量的体积贡献。

Result: 将自旋结构层对应关系推广到含Ramond标记点的自旋曲线；利用双曲描述构造特征形式和自旋测度，为模空间体积提供框架；发现偶与奇自旋分量体积贡献之间存在边界层贡献中不明显的对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了自旋曲线模空间中双曲几何与全纯几何之间的系统联系，并揭示了偶/奇自旋分量体积贡献的对称性，为后续应用Teichmüller方法研究模空间体积开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This article arose out of notes for a CIME summer school mini-course in Cetraro. In it we develop differential-geometric constructions on the moduli space ${\cal M}_{g,n}^{\rm spin}$ of smooth spin curves, with particular emphasis on their interpretation in hyperbolic geometry. We relate the locally constant sheaf arising from a hyperbolic spin structure to the corresponding holomorphic sheaf, extending this known relationship to spin curves with Ramond marked points. We use the hyperbolic description to construct characteristic forms and spin measures on moduli spaces of hyperbolic surfaces with geodesic boundary, providing a framework for applying Teichmüller-theoretic methods to their volumes and volume recursions. Finally, we study separately the even and odd components of spin moduli space and find symmetries between their volume contributions which are not apparent from the contributions of individual boundary strata.

</details>


### [168] [On monodromy of monodromy surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25594)
*Pieter Roffelsen,Alexander Stokes*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Painlevé方程的Riemann-Hilbert对应与其初始值空间的关系，证明了这些仿射簇（单值曲面）可嵌入实现为仿射del Pezzo曲面，并刻画了其单值群为相应Painlevé方程的仿射Weyl对称群的有限部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riemann-Hilbert对应将Painlevé方程的初始值空间与由相关线性ODE的单值不变量构造的仿射簇（单值曲面）联系起来。本文旨在深入理解这些仿射簇的几何结构及其对称群的代数性质。

Method: 对与Painlevé VI、IV、II、I方程相关的单值曲面，证明它们可实现为嵌入的仿射del Pezzo曲面（由其度和无穷远处给定的除子刻画）；从三个层面实现单值群：解析上作为参数空间中沿环路延拓诱导的直线置换，Galois理论上通过直线关联簇的函数域，组合上通过直线的交集图。

Result: 证明了这些单值曲面的单值群构成相应Painlevé方程仿射Weyl对称群的有限部分；单值群在解析、Galois理论和组合三个层面均得到实现；参数空间（模对称）可解释为嵌入仿射簇范畴的模空间；揭示了仿射Weyl群对称在经Riemann-Hilbert映射共轭后变得平凡，但有限Weyl群部分作为单值曲面的单值保留下来。

Conclusion: 单值曲面具有嵌入仿射del Pezzo曲面实现，其单值群保留了Painlevé方程仿射Weyl群对称的有限部分，这为参数空间提供了新的模解释，并揭示了Riemann-Hilbert对应下对称性保持的结构区分。

Abstract: Different instances of the Riemann-Hilbert correspondence relate initial value spaces of Painlevé equations to affine varieties known as monodromy surfaces, built from monodromy invariants for associated linear ODEs. We show that these affine varieties admit realisations as embedded affine del Pezzo surfaces, characterised by their degree and a prescribed divisor at infinity, first in this paper for cases associated with Painlevé equations $\rm{VI},\rm{IV},\rm{II},\rm{I}$. We prove that the monodromy groups of these monodromy surfaces form the finite parts of the affine Weyl symmetry groups of the corresponding Painlevé equations. We realise the monodromy group in each case: analytically as permutations of lines induced by continuation along loops in parameter space, Galois-theoretically in terms of the function field of the incidence variety of lines, and combinatorially via their intersection graph. This in particular yields an interpretation of the parameter spaces of Painlevé equations, modulo symmetries, as moduli spaces of categories of embedded affine varieties. Further, it shows that, despite the affine Weyl group symmetries becoming trivial when conjugated by the corresponding Riemann-Hilbert map, the finite Weyl group part survives as monodromy of the monodromy surface.

</details>


### [169] [The geography of Chern slopes with prescribed fundamental group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25306)
*Maycol Falla Luza*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有固定拓扑基本群G的极小一般型曲面S的Chern斜率c_1²/c_2的稠密性问题。Troncoso和Urzúa证明了这些斜率在[1,3]中稠密，但区间[1/3,1)未解决。本文证明斜率在[1/2,3]中稠密，同时证明了具有丰富典范类K_S的曲面的斜率在[1/2,2]中稠密。关键工具是一个精确的丰富性判据，它显示Troncoso-Urzúa乘积构造中的曲面永远不会具有丰富的典范类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Troncoso和Urzúa遗留的开放问题：即固定拓扑基本群G的极小一般型曲面Chern斜率在区间[1/3,1)上的稠密性。同时希望首次建立关于丰富典范类情况下斜率稠密性的Troncoso-Urzúa猜想。他们还希望检验并理解乘积构造中典范类的丰富性条件。

Method: 本文的方法包括：利用Mendes Lopes和Pardini的定理以及Reid猜想来确定区间下界[1/2,3]的最优性；利用乘积构造（product construction）结合精确的丰富性判据（ampleness criterion），该判据可用于判定构造曲面K_S的丰富性，并证明Troncoso-Urzúa构造中的曲面无论定义截面如何，其典范类永远不会丰富。

Result: 作者证明了：1) 对于固定基本群G的极小一般型曲面S，Chern斜率c_1²/c_2在[1/2,3]中稠密，且此区间不能扩大（受限于Mendes Lopes和Pardini的定理与Reid猜想）；2) 对于K_S丰富的此类曲面，斜率在[1/2,2]中稠密，这是Troncoso和Urzúa关于丰富典范类猜想首次被证实的情况。

Conclusion: 本文解决了Troncoso-Urzúa遗留问题的一部分，将稠密区间扩展到[1/2,3]，并首次在丰富典范类情形下证明了斜率稠密性。此外，精确的丰富性判据揭示了原构造中曲面典范类本质上不可能丰富，这对理解乘积构造的几何性质具有重要价值。

Abstract: Let $G$ be the topological fundamental group of a nonsingular complex projective surface. Troncoso and Urzúa proved that the Chern slopes $c_1^2/c_2$ of minimal surfaces of general type $S$ with $π_1(S)\simeq G$ are dense in $[1,3]$, and left $[1/3,1)$ open. We prove that they are dense in $[1/2,3]$, an interval that cannot be enlarged without contradicting either a theorem of Mendes Lopes and Pardini or Reid's conjecture. We prove more: the slopes of such surfaces with $K_S$ ample are dense in $[1/2,2]$, the first case in which the conjecture of Troncoso and Urzúa on ample canonical classes is established. The tool is an exact ampleness criterion for their product construction, which shows in particular that their own surfaces never have ample canonical class, whatever the defining sections.

</details>


### [170] [The image of the constant sheaf under the geometric Langlands equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25357)
*Kenta Suzuki*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文计算了光滑射影曲线上约化群G的Bun_G(X)上任意有限秩局部系统在几何Langlands对应下的像，并验证了V. Lafforgue在常值层情形下的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何Langlands对应是几何表示论的核心猜想，它建立了Bun_G(X)上的层与局部系统之间的对应关系。理解任意有限秩局部系统在该对应下的像对于深入理解几何Langlands对应的结构至关重要。V. Lafforgue提出了一个关于此像的具体猜想，本文旨在验证该猜想在常值层情形下成立。

Method: 本文采用几何Langlands对应的框架，利用代数叠和导出范畴的技术，通过分析Bun_G(X)上有限秩局部系统的特征与结构，在特征零代数闭域上对光滑射影曲线X进行计算，并将结果与Lafforgue的猜想进行对照验证。

Result: 成功地计算了Bun_G(X)上任意有限秩局部系统在几何Langlands等价下的像，并在常值层这一重要特殊情况中确认了V. Lafforgue猜想成立。

Conclusion: 本文的结果为几何Langlands对应提供了新的实质性证据，确认了Lafforgue关于常值层情形下的猜想，对理解几何Langlands对应的整体结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth projective curve and let $G$ be a reductive group over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero. We compute the image of arbitrary finite-rank local systems on $\mathrm{Bun}_G(X)$ under the geometric Langlands equivalence, confirming a conjecture of V. Lafforgue in the case of the constant sheaf.

</details>


### [171] [Quasimaps to Nakajima varieties as critical loci](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25938)
*Spencer Tamagni*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文观察到，从P^1到Nakajima quiver variety X的、将无穷远点送到给定点的拟映射（quasimaps）所构成的空间，可以被全局地表示为某个显式函数的临界轨迹（critical locus）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nakajima quiver variety在表示论和几何表示论中扮演核心角色，而拟映射空间是理解其模空间、量子群作用和GW/DT不变量等的重要工具。作者希望为拟映射空间提供一种全局的、显式的描述——即用某个函数的临界轨迹来表示，这有助于深化对这类空间几何结构的理解。

Method: 作者报告了一个观察（observation），即拟映射空间（quasimap moduli space）可以整体地呈现为某个显式函数的临界轨迹。这暗示使用临界轨迹（critical locus）的理论框架，可能有与Landau-Ginzburg模型或辫子范畴相关的结构。

Result: 给出了拟映射空间的全局描述：该空间同构于一个显式函数的临界轨迹，从而提供了一种新的表示方法。

Conclusion: 该观察为Nakajima quiver variety的拟映射空间提供了一种简洁而有力的全局描述，可能为进一步的几何与表示论应用（如稳定条件、模空间结构等）奠定基础。

Abstract: $X$ is a Nakajima quiver variety. In this note we report the observation that the space parameterizing quasimaps from $\mathbb{P}^1$ to $X$, sending infinity to a given point, may be presented globally as the critical locus of an explicit function.

</details>


### [172] [Topological String Blowup Equations via Stable Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25397)
*Lutian Zhao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对局部Hirzebruch三维簇Y_ℓ（ℓ=0,1,2）证明了双变量环面等变对称K-理论稳定对级数的blowup方程。除纤维类贡献后，四chart局部化逐系数地等同于框架秩二层模空间上(∨V)^ℓ的等变Euler特征级数。通过框架层blowup公式得到单位方程和消没方程；对ℓ=2这成为局部化指标等式。文中还陈述了条件性局部P²方程所需的两个猜想、一般Huang–Sun–Wang猜想，以及涉及E₈格的rational-elliptic特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在将blowup方程组推广到更一般的局部Hirzebruch三维簇（而非仅局部P²），并建立稳定对理论与框架秩二切丛模空间上的Euler特征之间的联系，从而为blowup方程的普适性提供更广泛的计算证据与理论基础。

Method: 作者利用四chart局部化技术，将稳定对级数除以纤维类贡献后，逐系数地等同为框架秩二丛模空间上的等变Euler特征级数（涉及(∨V)^ℓ）。借助框架层的blowup公式推导出unity方程与vanishing方程；对于ℓ=2的情形，该恒等式表现为局部化指标恒等式。

Result: 成功对0≤ℓ≤2的局部Hirzebruch三维簇证明了双变量对称K-理论稳定对级数的blowup方程，并通过框架层blowup公式得到unity与vanishing方程；ℓ=2时给出局部化指标恒等式。此外陈述了用于条件性局部P²推导的两个猜想及一般HSW猜想。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个系统框架，将稳定对级数的blowup方程转化为框架秩二层的指数恒等式；所提出的两个猜想与E₈格上的rational-elliptic特化为进一步推广到局部P²提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: For the local Hirzebruch threefolds \(Y_\ell=\operatorname{Tot}_{\mathbb F_\ell}K_{\mathbb F_\ell}\), \(0\leq\ell\leq2\), we prove blowup equations for the two-variable, torus-equivariant symmetrized \(K\)-theoretic stable-pair series. After division by the fibre-class contribution, four-chart localization is identified coefficientwise with the equivariant Euler-characteristic series of \((\det\mathcal V)^\ell\) on moduli spaces of framed rank-two sheaves, with \(\mathcal V\) tautological. The framed-sheaf blowup formulas then give the unity and vanishing equations; for \(\ell=2\) this is an identity of localized indices. We state separately the two conjectures needed for the conditional local \(\mathbb P^2\) equations, the general Huang--Sun--Wang conjecture, and a rational-elliptic specialization involving the \(E_8\) lattice.

</details>


### [173] [Quasi-Galois points for quartic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25445)
*Kei Miura,Shingo Taki*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了拟伽罗瓦点（quasi-Galois points），这是伽罗瓦点的推广。作者刻画了具有拟伽罗瓦点的光滑四次曲面与带特定对合的K3曲面之间的关系，给出了四次曲面拟伽罗瓦点的判定准则，并确定了Fermat四次曲面的所有拟伽罗瓦点（恰好28个）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽罗瓦点理论是代数几何中的重要研究对象，本文将其推广到拟伽罗瓦点，旨在更广泛地理解四次曲面上的对称性结构和投影性质。

Method: 利用K3曲面对合的理论来刻画光滑四次曲面上存在拟伽罗瓦点的充要条件，建立判定准则，并对Fermat四次曲面进行具体的拟伽罗瓦点分类计算。

Result: 得到了光滑四次曲面具有拟伽罗瓦点的K3曲面刻画与判定准则；确定了Fermat四次曲面恰好有28个拟伽罗瓦点。

Conclusion: 本文建立了拟伽罗瓦点与K3曲面对合之间的深刻联系，并完整解决了Fermat四次曲面上拟伽罗瓦点的分类问题。

Abstract: We study quasi-Galois points, which are a generalization of Galois points. We characterize smooth quartic surfaces admitting a quasi-Galois point in terms of K3 surfaces with a certain involution, and provide a criterion for quasi-Galois points for quartic surfaces. Moreover, we also determine all quasi-Galois points of the Fermat quartic surface and show that it has exactly 28 quasi-Galois points.

</details>


### [174] [Tautological Pushforwards of Hilbert Schemes of Points on Curves and Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25447)
*Bochao Kong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于Hilbert概形上tautological积分的相对普适性定理：将Ellingsrud–Göttsche–Lehn的经典普适性结果推广到带基空间B的相对情形，其中tautological pushforward由普适幂级数与相对κ-类控制。作为应用，作者确定了曲线族和曲面族上总Segre pushforward的所有普适系数，其中曲线情形的递推关系与单调Hurwitz数相关，解决了Oprea–Pandharipande提出的问题；曲面情形得到了Lehn猜想的一个余维一相对形式，并给出了每个普适级数的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ellingsrud–Göttsche–Lehn证明了Hilbert概形上tautological积分的普适性定理，但该结果仅适用于绝对情形（即基空间为点）。本文的动机是将该普适性推广到相对情形（即带任意基空间B），从而能够处理曲线族和曲面族等更一般几何对象上的问题。同时，这也响应了Oprea–Pandharipande关于Segre类的具体问题，并受到Marian–Oprea–Pandharipande关于Lehn猜想的启发。

Method: 本文建立了一个相对形式的普适性定理：在基空间B上，tautological pushforward由普适幂级数与相对κ-类的配对所控制。在应用部分，作者针对曲线族和曲面族分别计算了总Segre pushforward的所有普适系数；对于曲面族，利用余维一相对结构对Lehn猜想进行了相对推广，并为每个普适级数推导出显式公式。

Result: 对于曲线族，所有总Segre pushforward的普适系数被完全确定，所得递推关系与单调Hurwitz数之间存在出人意料的联系，并回答了Oprea–Pandharipande的问题。对于曲面族，获得了Lehn猜想的余维一相对形式，且每个普适级数都有显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了tautological积分普适性定理的相对推广，建立了相对κ-类框架下的完整理论，并在曲线和曲面两类重要情形中给出了完全显式的结果，同时揭示了与单调Hurwitz数之间的新联系。

Abstract: Ellingsrud, Göttsche, and Lehn proved a remarkable universality result for tautological integrals on Hilbert schemes of points on surfaces. We prove a relative form: over a base $B$, the tautological pushforwards are governed by universal power series paired with relative $κ$-classes.
  As applications, we focus on Segre classes on families of curves and surfaces. For curve families, we determine all universal coefficients for total Segre pushforwards. These formulas answer a question of Oprea--Pandharipande, and the resulting recursions are surprisingly related to monotone Hurwitz numbers. For surface families, motivated by Marian--Oprea--Pandharipande's work on Lehn's conjecture, we obtain a codimension-one relative form of the conjecture, with explicit formulas for every universal series.

</details>


### [175] [Multiplicity-One Cox Rings of Toric Point Blow-Ups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25554)
*Antonio Laface,Luca Ugaglia*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究完全环面簇X在其开环面单位点爆破后的Cox环结构，给出了Rees重数一生成的有限判别准则，并将其应用于伪加权射影空间和射影环面曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于完全环面簇X在开环面单位点e处的爆破Bl_eX，其Cox环由环面点格理想I_X的饱和幂次描述。作者希望建立关于Rees重数一生成的有限判别准则，从而系统性地判断这类Cox环是否由一次生成（即Rees重数为一），这是交换代数和代数几何中的经典问题。

Method: 通过投影格理想的解析展形（analytic spreads）和相关分次环中的零因子来给出Rees重数一生成的有限判别准则。具体而言，该准则涉及投影格理想的解析展形以及相关分次环中的零因子结构。

Result: (1) 对伪加权射影空间，证明了重数一生成等价于I_X为完全交；(2) 对射影环面曲面，证明了重数一生成当且仅当X所支配的每个Picard数为一的环面曲面都满足重数一生成。

Conclusion: 该工作将环面簇爆破的Cox环生成问题归结为格理想的代数性质，给出了可操作的有限判别条件，并在两类重要情形（伪加权射影空间与射影环面曲面）中完全刻画了重数一生成的条件。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a complete toric variety and let $e$ be the identity of its open torus. The Cox ring of ${\rm Bl}_eX$ is described by saturated powers of the toric-point lattice ideal $I_X$. We give a finite criterion for generation in Rees multiplicity one in terms of analytic spreads of projected lattice ideals and zero divisors in the associated graded ring. We apply this criterion first to fake weighted projective spaces, proving that multiplicity-one generation is equivalent to $I_X$ being a complete intersection. For projective toric surfaces, we prove that multiplicity-one generation holds if and only if it holds for every Picard-number-one toric surface dominated by $X$.

</details>


### [176] [On the Multiplicative Thom-Sebastiani Property for Bernstein-Sato Polynomials of Ideals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25576)
*Yongxin Xu,Huaiqing Zuo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了理想的Bernstein-Sato多项式满足乘法Thom-Sebastiani性质，肯定地回答了Shi和Zuo提出的问题，该问题是对Budur和Popa独立提出的问题的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿用Shi和Zuo的问题，该问题推广了Budur和Popa独立提出的问题，旨在理解理想的Bernstein-Sato多项式在乘法卷积下的行为。

Method: 通过严格的数学证明，展示了理想的Bernstein-Sato多项式满足乘法Thom-Sebastiani性质。

Result: 成功证明了理想Bernstein-Sato多项式的乘法Thom-Sebastiani性质成立，从而肯定地回答了Shi和Zuo的问题。

Conclusion: 理想的Bernstein-Sato多项式在乘法Thom-Sebastiani运算下具有良性的结构性质，为相关领域的进一步研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We prove that the Bernstein-Sato polynomials of ideals satisfy the multiplicative Thom-Sebastiani property. This affirmatively settles a question of Shi and Zuo that extends a question independently formulated by Budur and Popa.

</details>


### [177] [The platonic elliptic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25644)
*Nutsa Gegelia,Duco van Straten*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了与正十二面体（柏拉图立体）自然相关的一类特殊有理椭圆曲面，具有四个约化奇异纤维，属于补充半稳定Beauville曲面的一个曲面族。描述了这些曲面的代数几何性质、Picard-Fuchs算子、整数序列、Laurent多项式表示和Apéry常数。在算术方面，发现需要对用于寻找纤维Euler因子的Dwork展开进行精化，这通过比较椭圆曲线的q坐标与Picard-Fuchs方程的q坐标来解释，并直接反映在Frobenius基中单值矩阵的形状上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注半稳定Beauville曲面，但对与柏拉图立体相关的特殊有理椭圆曲面（具有四个约化奇异纤维）缺乏系统性研究。作者旨在构造并刻画这类曲面，同时揭示其在算术方面（特别是Dwork展开求解Euler因子时）的独特现象，从而补充和深化对这类椭圆纤维化曲面的理解。

Method: 首先基于柏拉图立体构造特定的有理椭圆曲面（具有四个约化奇异纤维），随后系统研究其代数几何结构，推导相关的Picard-Fuchs微分算子、整数序列及Laurent多项式表示。在算术方面，通过比较椭圆曲线上的q坐标与Picard-Fuchs方程的q坐标，分析Dwork展开在求解纤维Euler因子时失效的原因，并提出精化方案；同时通过Frobenius基中单值矩阵的形状验证该精化的必要性。

Result: 成功构造了一组与柏拉图立体相关且补充半稳定Beauville曲面的特殊有理椭圆曲面族，给出了其完整的代数几何性质与Picard-Fuchs算子刻画。发现标准的Dwork展开不足以正确获取这类纤维化的Euler因子，需要精化；该精化由椭圆曲线q坐标与Picard-Fuchs方程q坐标的差异所驱动，并在Frobenius基下单值矩阵的具体形状中得到体现。

Conclusion: 这类与柏拉图立体相关的新曲面族在几何拓扑与算术两方面都展现出丰富而独特的结构。Dwork展开需要精化这一发现表明，对于具有非半稳定纤维的椭圆曲面积分理论，标准方法需加以调整，且这一调整可通过q坐标的比较和单值矩阵的结构得到自洽的解释。

Abstract: We construct certain special rational elliptic surfaces with four reduced singular fibres that are naturally attached to the platonic solids and belong to a group of surfaces complementing the well-known semi-stable Beauville surfaces. We describe the basic algebraic geometric properties of these surfaces, their associated Picard-Fuchs operators, integer sequences, Laurent polynomial representations and Apéry constants. On the arithmetic side, we discover the need for a refinement of the Dwork-expansion used to find the Euler factors of the fibres of these fibrations. This is explained by comparing the $q$-coordinates coming from the elliptic curve and the $q$-coordinate of the Picard-Fuchs equation and is also directly reflected in the shape of the monodromy matrices in the Frobenius basis.

</details>


### [178] [The Moduli Space of Determinantal Representations of Cubic Surfaces and Invariant Theory of Root Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25695)
*Patrick Omukuba*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个框架，用于研究含奇异点的复三次曲面族在奇点边界上的线性行列式表示模空间。先处理具有E6型有理双奇点的经典形变情形，通过对有限分歧伽罗瓦覆盖建立全局同时消解，将相对模空间实现为对角Weyl商。随后扩展到含E~6型简单椭圆奇点的临界边界情形，因局部单值群变为无限群而经典的消解框架失效，转而利用Looijenga仿射Weyl群不变代数构造几何替代Z，证明模空间与Z的全空间全局同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典形变理论仅适用于有理双奇点（如E6型）情形，当曲面向临界边界退化出现E~6型简单椭圆奇点时，局部单值群变为无限群，经典的同时消解框架完全失效，需要新的几何工具来刻画模空间结构。

Method: （1）对E6情形：在全局参数切片上建立有限分歧伽罗瓦覆盖，通过全局同时消解实现对相对模空间H_N的刻画，将其实现为对角Weyl商(h×R)/W(E6)；（2）对E~6情形：利用Looijenga仿射Weyl群不变代数的滤过构造几何替代Z，证明Z是结构椭圆曲线E上的向量丛，且分解为次数恰好等于E6最高根负Coxeter标号之和的线丛直和；最后利用Riemann延拓定理和椭圆曲线上向量丛的刚性证明模空间与Z全空间全局同构。

Result: 建立了跨越E6型有理双奇点与E~6型简单椭圆奇点边界情形的线性行列式表示模空间分类；在前者中实现为对角Weyl商，在后者中证明半泛族模空间全局同构于由Looijenga不变代数构造的向量丛Z的全空间，其分解次数与E6最高根的负Coxeter标号精确匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地统一了经典有理奇点与超越性椭圆奇点两种边界情形下的模空间描述，表明当经典分解框架失效时，仿射Weyl群的不变代数结构为模空间提供了自然的替代几何描述。

Abstract: We present a framework for studying the moduli space of linear determinantal representations for flat families of complex projective cubic surfaces across singularity boundaries. First, we deal with the classical deformation theory where a surface S_0 possesses a rational double point (RDP) of type E_6. By establishing a global simultaneous resolution over a finite ramified Galois covering of the global parameter slice, we realize the relative moduli space H_N as a diagonal Weyl quotient (h x R)/W(E_6). Second, we extend this classification to the critical boundary configuration where S0 contains a unique isolated simple elliptic singularity of type E~_6. Because the local monodromy group becomes infinite, the classical simultaneous resolution framework completely breaks down. We bypass this obstruction by constructing a geometric substitute Z derived from the filtration of Looijenga's invariant algebra of affine Weyl groups. We also show that the vector bundle Z decomposes into a direct sum of line bundles over the structural elliptic curve E, with degrees explicitly matching the negative Coxeter marks of the highest root of E_6. Utilizing Riemann's extension theorem and vector bundle rigidity over elliptic curves, we settle our isomorphism result, proving that the moduli space H-bar representing the semiuniversal family is globally isomorphic to the total space of Z.

</details>


### [179] [Uniform non-homogeneous bundles on quadrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25921)
*Xinyi Fang,Yuhang Zhou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究了广义Grassmann流形上一致丛的齐次性问题。证明了除射影空间Pn外，n维广义Grassmann流形X满足k(X)≤n-1，其中k(X)是保证所有秩不超过该值的均匀丛都是齐次丛的最大整数。特别地，对光滑二次曲面Q^n，奇数维时k(Q^n)=n-1，偶数维时n-2≤k(Q^n)≤n-1。此外，分类了n=3,5时Q^n上的秩n均匀丛，并用均匀丛的性质刻画了广义Grassmann流形中的射影空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义Grassmann流形上均匀丛（uniform bundle）的齐次性（homogeneity）问题，即确定哪些秩范围内的均匀丛必然是齐次丛，并尝试通过均匀丛性质来区分射影空间与其他广义Grassmann流形。

Method: 利用代数几何与向量丛理论，对广义Grassmann流形上均匀丛的秩上限k(X)进行估计。通过构造和分析非齐次均匀丛来给出上界，并通过具体分类方法确定光滑二次曲面上的均匀丛结构，结合低维情形（n=3,5）的细致分类完成证明。

Result: 得到k(X)≤n-1的一般上界；对于光滑二次曲面，奇维时精确为n-1，偶维时给出区间n-2≤k(Q^n)≤n-1；分类了n=3,5时Q^n上的秩n均匀丛；并利用均匀丛刻画了广义Grassmann流形中的射影空间。

Conclusion: 广义Grassmann流形上均匀丛的齐次性与流形的维数和结构密切相关，射影空间在此类问题中具有特殊地位，可以通过均匀丛是否存在非齐次情形的性质加以区分。

Abstract: Let $X$ be an $n$-dimensional generalized Grassmannian not isomorphic to $\mathbb{P}^n$. We prove that $k(X)\le n-1$, where $k(X)$ denotes the maximal integer such that every uniform bundle on $X$ of rank at most $k(X)$ is homogeneous. In particular, for smooth quadrics $\mathbb{Q}^n$, we have $k(\mathbb{Q}^n)=n-1$ for odd $n$, and $n-2\le k(\mathbb{Q}^n)\le n-1$ for even $n$. We classify uniform rank $n$ bundles on $\mathbb{Q}^{n}$ for $n=3$, $5$. Furthermore, we characterize projective spaces among generalized Grassmannians in terms of uniform bundles.

</details>


### [180] [Cyclic covers via mixed Hodge modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25947)
*Scott Hiatt*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了复代数簇（不必光滑）上任意线丛的N重循环覆盖，并用Morihiko Saito的混合Hodge模理论来描述这种循环覆盖的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环覆盖是代数几何中的基本构造，但以往的研究通常假设底空间X是光滑的。本文旨在将循环覆盖的描述推广到非光滑的复代数簇情形，并利用Saito的混合Hodge模理论这一强大工具来获得更一般的描述。

Method: 给定复代数簇X上的线丛L，存在全局截面s定义了有效Cartier除子D。通过截面s构造N重循环覆盖π:Y→X，然后利用Morihiko Saito的混合Hodge模理论对该覆盖进行描述与分析。

Result: 成功地在非光滑情形下，用混合Hodge模的语言描述了由有效Cartier除子诱导的N重循环覆盖的结构。

Conclusion: 循环覆盖可以在一般（非光滑）复代数簇上通过混合Hodge模理论得到统一的描述，这为后续研究提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Suppose we are given an arbitrary line bundle $\mathcal{L}$ on a complex algebraic variety $X$, not necessarily smooth. For a positive integer $N$, suppose there exists a global section $s \in Γ(X, \mathcal{L}^{N})$ that defines an effective Cartier divisor $D$. If we denote $π: Y \rightarrow X$ to be the $N$-fold cyclic covering of the divisor $D$ resulting from the global section $s$, we describe the cyclic covering in terms of Morihiko Saito's theory of mixed Hodge modules.

</details>


### [181] [When are tropical multidegrees positive?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25987)
*Yairon Cid-Ruiz*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含在实向量空间乘积中的热带簇的"热带多重次数"的正性。通过将热带簇与正热带除子的拉回稳定相交得到多重次数，作者引入了"投影纯性"（projection-purity）和"面可选择性"（facet-selectability）两个条件，在这两个条件下，正性由自然投影的维度决定，且热带多重次数的支撑集恰为某个多拟阵基多胞形的格点集合。这推广了He关于平移可容许热带簇的定理。作者还证明这些条件本身并不保证相应的热带体积多项式是Lorentzian的；相反，对于任何多拟阵的增广Bergman扇，正多重次数恰好在多拟阵基多胞形的格点上得到支撑，且对任意正热带除子序列，热带体积多项式均为Lorentzian。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解热带簇在实向量空间乘积中的多重次数的正性条件，并推广已有的He定理（针对平移可容许热带簇）。同时探究这些条件与Lorentzian性质之间的关系，以深化热带几何中正性与组合结构（如多拟阵）的联系。

Method: 引入投影纯性与面可选择性两个新条件；通过稳定相交技术将热带多重次数与正热带除子的拉回联系起来；利用多拟阵基多胞形与增广Bergman扇的组合结构刻画热带多重次数的支撑集，并研究相应的热带体积多项式的Lorentzian性质。

Result: 在投影纯性和面可选择性条件下，热带多重次数的正性完全由自然投影维度决定，且其支撑集恰为多拟阵基多胞形的格点集合，推广了He定理。但这两个条件本身不足以使热带体积多项式成为Lorentzian。对于增广Bergman扇，正多重次数精确支撑在多拟阵基多胞形的格点上，且对所有正热带除子序列，热带体积多项式均为Lorentzian。

Conclusion: 投影纯性和面可选择性给出热带多重次数正性的充分组合刻画，但需要更强的结构（如增广Bergman扇）才能保证Lorentzian性质。这揭示了一般的正性条件与更强的代数性质（Lorentzian）之间存在本质差距。

Abstract: We study the positivity of the tropical multidegrees of a tropical variety contained in a product of real vector spaces. These multidegrees are obtained by stably intersecting the tropical variety with pullbacks of positive tropical divisors. We introduce projection-purity and facet-selectability, two conditions under which positivity is determined by the dimensions of the natural projections, and the support of the tropical multidegrees is precisely the set of lattice points of a polymatroid base polytope. This extends He's theorem for translation-admissible tropical varieties. We also show that these conditions alone do not force the corresponding tropical volume polynomial to be Lorentzian. By contrast, for the augmented Bergman fan of any polymatroid, the positive multidegrees are supported precisely on the lattice points of the polymatroid base polytope, and the tropical volume polynomial is Lorentzian for every sequence of positive tropical divisors.

</details>


### [182] [Crepant partial resolutions of the nilpotent cone via Hamiltonian reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25994)
*Gwyn Bellamy,Tom Gannon*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了单连通半单代数群的幂零锥及其所有crepant射影部分解可以通过仿射闭包的基本仿射空间余切丛在极大环面的稳定参数下做Hamiltonian约化得到，并进一步实现了这些解的全纯Poisson形变的基变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幂零锥及其crepant射影部分解在代数几何和表示论中具有核心地位，研究者希望找到一种统一的构造方式，将这类几何对象与Hamiltonian约化和GIT商这两类经典构造联系起来，从而为研究其Poisson形变提供统一框架。

Method: 方法核心是利用基本仿射空间余切丛的仿射闭包，配合一个极大环面的稳定参数选取。通过选择不同的稳定性参数，分别构造幂零锥（及其各crepant射影部分解）作为Hamiltonian约化的结果，并将全纯Poisson形变的基变换实现为相应稳定参数下的GIT商。

Result: 成功地将单连通半单代数群的幂零锥、其所有crepant射影部分解，以及这些解的universal Poisson形变的基变换，统一地刻画为基本仿射空间余切丛仿射闭包在极大环面不同稳定参数下的Hamiltonian约化或GIT商。

Conclusion: 该结果表明幂零锥及其相关几何对象与Hamiltonian约化之间存在本质联系，稳定参数的选取提供了统一且系统的框架，为后续研究这些crepant部分解的形变理论、量子化及相关不变量奠定了基础。

Abstract: We show that the nilpotent cone associated to a simply connected semisimple algebraic group, together with all its crepant projective partial resolutions, can be constructed as Hamiltonian reductions of the affine closure of the cotangent bundle of base affine space for suitable choices of stability parameter of a maximal torus. We also realize the base change of the universal Poisson deformation of each of these crepant projective partial resolutions as the GIT quotient of the affine closure of the cotangent bundle of base affine space at the same stability parameter.

</details>


### [183] [Generalised Cone Conjecture, I: Beyond Calabi--Yau](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26079)
*Vladimir Lazić,Isabel Stenger,Zhixin Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是系列论文的第一篇，旨在证明曲面上的广义锥猜想（Generalised Cone Conjecture）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义锥猜想是对经典锥定理的一般化推广，作者试图在二维曲面情形下建立该猜想的理论依据。

Method: 由于摘要仅提供了引言性说明，具体方法需参考论文全文，但可以推断作者采用了代数几何中与锥定理相关的技术手段。

Result: 摘要未提供具体结果，表明本文为系列论文的开篇，重点在于搭建证明框架。

Conclusion: 本文是该系列论文的第一部分，后续将给出广义锥猜想在曲面上的完整证明。

Abstract: This is the first of two papers in which we prove the Generalised Cone Conjecture on surfaces.

</details>


### [184] [Negative Effective Divisors and Bridgeland Stability of Line Bundles on Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26080)
*Anthony Mäkelä*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在连通光滑复射影曲面 X 上证明了 Arcara–Miles 猜想的有效除子版本及其严格版本。对每个除子类 Bridgeland 稳定性条件，线丛或其相关平移的不稳定（或半不稳定）可通过与非零有效 Cartier 除子 C（满足 C²<0）相关的自然子对象来检测。证明结合了最小秩不稳定子、斜率 Harder–Narasimhan 滤过、Bogomolov–Gieseker 不等式，以及一个有序 Lorentz 偏和估计（迫使最小秩为 1）。由此推出：线丛或其相关平移的严格半稳定性可由负有效除子检测，且由 twisted deformed Hermitian–Yang–Mills 方程产生的数值半稳定性条件等价于在全体积分伸缩下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arcara–Miles 猜想是曲面 Bridgeland 稳定性理论中的一个重要猜想，它断言线丛的不稳定性可由某种几何条件（与负自交除子相关）来检测。作者的目标是证明该猜想的有效除子版本，并建立其严格版本，从而将抽象稳定性判据转化为可由负有效 Cartier 除子直接刻画的几何判据。

Method: 证明方法包括四个关键成分：(1) 采用最小秩不稳定子来约化问题；(2) 利用斜率 Harder–Narasimhan 滤过分析除子在上述滤过中的比较位置；(3) 应用 Bogomolov–Gieseker 不等式控制相关数值不变量；(4) 构造一个有序 Lorentz 偏和估计，利用其符号性质迫使最小秩必须为 1，从而将问题转化为可处理的有效除子情形。

Result: 证明了 Arcara–Miles 猜想的有效除子版本及其严格版本：对任意除子类 Bridgeland 稳定性条件，线束或其相关移位的（半）不稳定性均可由满足 C²<0 的非零有效 Cartier 除子 C 对应的自然子对象检测。进一步推出严格半稳定性可由负有效除子检测，并将 twisted deformed Hermitian–Yang–Mills 方程诱导的数值半稳定性条件等价于所有积分伸缩下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了线丛在曲面 Bridgeland 稳定性下的完全几何刻画，将稳定性判定归约为负自交有效除子这一经典几何对象，并建立了与 twisted deformed Hermitian–Yang–Mills 方程之间的联系，深化了对曲面派生范畴稳定性结构与微分几何方程之间关系的理解。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a connected smooth complex projective surface. We prove an effective-divisor version of the Arcara--Miles conjecture, together with its strict analogue. For every divisorial Bridgeland stability condition, failure of stability, respectively semistability, of a line bundle or its relevant shift is detected by a natural subobject associated with a non-zero effective Cartier divisor $C$ satisfying $C^2<0$.
  The proof combines minimal-rank destabilizers, slope Harder--Narasimhan filtrations, the Bogomolov--Gieseker inequality, and an ordered Lorentzian partial-sum estimate that forces the minimal rank to be one. Consequently, strict semistability of a line bundle or its relevant shift is detected by a negative effective divisor, and a numerical semistability condition arising from the twisted deformed Hermitian--Yang--Mills equation is equivalent to stability under all integral scalings.

</details>


### [185] [The Multivariable Strong Monodromy Conjecture for Plane Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26087)
*Sheng Tan*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文针对光滑复空间胚上的全纯函数元组F，发展了一种迭代留数障碍（iterated-residue obstruction），证明非零系数值的留数类在SNC（简单正规交叉）层位上存在时，相应的精确仿射参数必然落在Bernstein-Sato理想B_{F,0}的零点集Z(B_{F,0})中。作为应用，证明了局部多元拓扑zeta函数的每个最大阶极超平面都包含于Bernstein-Sato零点集，且对约化平面曲线奇点元组的所有实际极超平面同样成立，从而证明了平面曲线的拓扑多元强单值猜想（Strong Monodromy Conjecture）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernstein-Sato理想与拓扑zeta函数的极结构之间的关系是奇点理论中的核心问题。经典单值猜想（Monodromy Conjecture）探讨拓扑zeta函数的极点与特征值的关系，而强单值猜想进一步要求极点对应Bernstein-Sato多项式的根。多元推广中，需要建立多元Bernstein-Sato零点集与多元拓扑zeta函数极超平面之间的精确联系，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者引入迭代留数障碍技术：在SNC层的解析化（arc resolution）上，构造系数值的留数类（residue class），并证明每个非零的留数类所对应的精确仿射参数量必然落在Bernstein-Sato理想的零点集内。这一方法结合了留数微积分、D-模理论和奇点消解技术，从局部留数结构直接导出Bernstein-Sato理想零点的包含关系。

Result: 1) 证明了每个最大阶极超平面（maximal-order polar hyperplane）包含于Bernstein-Sato零点集Z(B_{F,0})；2) 对约化平面曲线芽元组F，证明了每个实际极超平面（actual polar hyperplane）也包含于Z(B_{F,0})；3) 由此推出了平面曲线情形的拓扑多元强单值猜想。

Conclusion: 通过迭代留数障碍这一新工具，作者成功将多元拓扑zeta函数的极结构与Bernstein-Sato理想的零点集建立起了严格的包含关系。特别地，在平面曲线情形完整证明了多元强单值猜想，为更一般情形的强单值猜想提供了新的方法论基础和技术路径。

Abstract: Let $F=(f_1,\ldots,f_r)$ be a tuple of holomorphic germs on a smooth complex germ, and let $B_{F,0}$ be its Bernstein--Sato ideal. We develop an iterated-residue obstruction showing that a nonzero coefficient-valued residue class on an SNC stratum forces the corresponding exact affine parameter to lie in $Z(B_{F,0})$. As applications, we prove that every maximal-order polar hyperplane of the local multivariable topological zeta function is contained in the Bernstein--Sato zero locus, and that the same holds for every actual polar hyperplane associated with a tuple of reduced plane curve germs. The latter proves the topological multivariable Strong Monodromy Conjecture for plane curves.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Common-Center Geometry and Certified Radial Reconstruction for Energy-Form Full Conformal Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24964)
*Yiheng Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于能量形式成对评分的全保形预测（FullCP）区域的几何性质，证明了在对称性、恒定对角线和Fréchet型目标可达到等条件下，保形预测区域关于公共最小化点为星形；并给出了幂距离和单变量CRPS情形的具体几何结论，以及通过Lipschitz控制获得内外径向包络的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中关于保形预测区域拓扑结构（如连通性、凸性）的几何保证不足，即使候选评分是凸损失的经验平均也不能保证FullCP区域连通。作者希望系统地刻画能量形式成对评分下保形预测区域的可验证几何性质，为后续的边界搜索和径向包络算法提供理论基础。

Method: 通过对留一法（leave-one-out）评分的直接展开，将每个训练点比较转化为成对不相似性子水平条件；在对称性、恒定对角线和Fréchet型目标可达性条件下证明所有比较区域包含公共最小化点；利用凸性和星形性质推导保形预测区域的几何结构；并针对幂距离ρ_β和单变量CRPS特例给出明确的导数界以建立Lipschitz控制，结合方向根搜索与经典Lipschitz延拓技术构造内外径向包络。

Result: 在所列条件下，非平凡精确保形区域关于公共最小化点为星形；对幂距离β≥1时确定性几何成立（而能量评分在0<β<2严格正确）；单变量β=1情形下每个非平凡经验CRPS的FullCP区域是非空闭区间；在1<β<2且m≥2的重构范围内，显式可检验的导数界给出比较集径向出口的Lipschitz控制，得到的径向包络宽度至多为δ+2Lh_U，并具有同射线Hausdorff保证。二维解析例子说明了保留星形而非凸性的几何价值。

Conclusion: 本文建立了能量形式成对评分下保形预测区域的确定性几何理论，表明星形而非凸性是更贴切且可验证的结构性质。该方法的适用范围面向低维多元输出场景的重构视角，而非高维尺度化或运行时间优化。

Abstract: This note studies the geometry of full conformal prediction (FullCP) regions generated by an empirical energy-form pairwise score. Candidate-score convexity alone does not guarantee connected FullCP regions, even when the candidate score is an empirical average of a loss convex in its first argument. Direct expansion of the leave-one-out scores shows that each training-point comparison for the energy-form score is exactly a pairwise-dissimilarity sublevel condition. Under symmetry, a constant diagonal, a diagonal lower bound, and attainment of the associated Fréchet-type objective, every comparison region contains a common minimizer; when the comparison regions are convex, the nontrivial exact conformal region is therefore star-shaped about that same point. For power distances $ρ_β(x,y)=\|x-y\|^β$, this deterministic geometry holds for $β\ge1$, while the conventional energy score is strictly proper for $0<β<2$. In the univariate $β=1$ specialization, every nontrivial empirical-CRPS FullCP region is a nonempty closed interval, possibly $\mathbb R$ in the $m=1$ degeneracy. On the unconditional reconstruction range $1<β<2$ and $m\ge2$, explicit data-checkable derivative bounds yield Lipschitz control of the comparison-set radial exits and hence of the exact conformal radial function. These score-specific bounds permit existing directional root-search ideas and classical Lipschitz-extension machinery to yield certified inner and outer radial envelopes with width at most $δ+2Lh_{\mathcal U}$ and corresponding same-ray Hausdorff guarantees. An analytic two-dimensional example shows why retaining star-shaped but nonconvex geometry can matter. The resulting reconstruction perspective is intended for low-dimensional multivariate outputs rather than high-dimensional scaling or runtime improvement.

</details>


### [187] [Improved Analysis for Hessian-free High-resolution Monte Carlo Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25052)
*Wujun Lv,Xiaoyu Wang,Yingli Wang,Lingjiong Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hessian-free高分辨率(HFHR)动力学及其蒙特卡洛算法(HFHRMC)的收敛性质，建立了显式的收缩率与非渐近收敛界，并证明了在非凸势函数下，引入正的位置扩散参数α能提升采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中的采样问题面临着高维、非凸势函数等挑战。欠阻尼Langevin动力学(ULD)是常用工具，但计算Hessian矩阵成本高昂。HFHR动力学通过引入可逆位置扩散来增强ULD，但缺乏严格的定量收敛分析。本文旨在建立HFHR动力学的显式收缩率和非渐近收敛保证。

Method: 在位置Poincaré不等式、加权Hessian和Laplacian界以及紧致Sobolev嵌入条件下，推导HFHR动力学的显式定量收缩率；通过时间增广Poincaré不等式获得比ULD更优的速率；利用弱解构造和自包含的散度引理光谱证明；对HFHRMC算法采用路径空间Girsanov论证获得全变差距离下的非渐近收敛界和迭代复杂度。

Result: 建立了对所有α≥0和γ>0成立的显式迭代复杂度界，且在ULD端点保持正则性。优化复杂度界得到有限精度下正的位置扩散参数，其高精度主导阶与优化后的ULD端点一致。迭代复杂度界优于现有HFHR算法工作，数值实验验证了正α的效益。

Conclusion: HFHR动力学在非凸势下具有严格的收敛保证，正的位置扩散参数α在实际有限精度下能提升采样效率，同时在高精度极限下不牺牲渐近最优性，为机器学习采样提供了一种兼具理论保证和实际效果的算法。

Abstract: Hessian-free high-resolution (HFHR) dynamics augments underdamped Langevin dynamics (ULD) with reversible position diffusion for sampling problems that arise in machine learning. We establish an explicit quantitative contraction rate for HFHR dynamics under a position Poincaré inequality, weighted Hessian and Laplacian bounds, and a compact Sobolev embedding, where the potential function is not necessarily convex. An adapted time-augmented Poincaré inequality yields an explicit rate that improves upon the contraction rate of the underdamped Langevin dynamics. We also give a weak-solution construction and a self-contained spectral proof of the divergence lemma underlying the argument. For HFHR Monte Carlo (HFHRMC) algorithm, which is based on a discretization scheme of HFHR dynamics, we use a path-space Girsanov argument to obtain a non-asymptotic convergence bound and an explicit iteration complexity in total variation distance. The bounds hold for every $α\geq0$ and $γ>0$ and remain regular at the ULD endpoint. Optimizing the iteration complexity bound yields a positive, accuracy-dependent position-diffusion parameter at finite accuracy, while its leading high-accuracy order coincides with that of the optimized ULD endpoint. Our iteration complexity bound improves upon the existing work on HFHR algorithms. Numerical experiments including Bayesian learning problems on real data are provided to illustrate the effect of positive $α$ and its benefit.

</details>


### [188] [Minimax Alternating Regret for the Experts Problem and Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25182)
*Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线凸优化（OCO）中交替遗憾（alternating regret）的极小极大最优率问题。对于d-expert问题，极小极大交替遗憾为Θ(log d)，与时间T无关；对于一般OCO，极小极大交替遗憾为Θ(d log(1+T/d))。上界通过修正的Hedge算法（含精心设计的校正项）实现，下界通过专家消除和单位圆盘上的多尺度构造实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替学习动力学在二人博弈中取得了成功，但在OCO中交替遗憾的极小极大最优率此前一直是开放问题。尽管已有工作表明在多种假设下可实现o(√T)的交替遗憾，但即便对于专家问题，该最优率也长期未解。Cevher等人[2023]和Hait等人[2025]明确提出了这一开放问题。

Method: 上界方面：专家问题使用一种修正的Hedge算法变体，通过精心设计的校正项消除交替遗憾分析中出现的曲率项；该校正势能论证被扩展到连续动作集。下界方面：专家问题构造通过反复消除一半候选专家来实现；一般OCO的下界实例构造则在单位圆盘上使用更复杂的多尺度构造替代离散消除。

Result: 1) 对d-expert问题，极小极大交替遗憾为Θ(log d)，与T无关，显著优于此前已知的O(T^{1/3} log^{2/3} d)；2) 对一般d维紧凸集上的OCO，最坏情形极小极大交替遗憾为Θ(d log(1+T/d))，显著优于此前已知的O((d log T)^{2/3} T^{1/3})上界。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了交替遗憾在专家问题和一般OCO中的极小极大最优率，匹配了上下界，并解决了Cevher等人[2023]和Hait等人[2025]提出的开放问题。结果表明交替遗憾在专家问题中不依赖于时间T，展现了交替学习动力学更优的理论保证。

Abstract: In this paper, we study alternating regret in online convex optimization (OCO), motivated by the success of alternating learning dynamics in two-player games. Although previous works have shown that $o(\sqrt{T})$ alternating regret is achievable under various assumptions on the loss functions and feasible domains, the minimax regret rate has remained open even for the expert problem. In this paper, we resolve this question by showing matching lower and upper bounds for both the expert problem and general OCO. Somewhat surprisingly, for the $d$-expert problem, we show that the minimax alternating regret is $Θ(\log d)$, independent of the horizon $T$. This significantly improves upon the best-known $\mathcal{O}(T^{1/3}\log^{2/3} d)$ established by Hait et al. [2025]. We further extend our results to general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set and prove that the worst-case minimax alternating regret is $Θ\left(d\log \left(1+\frac{T}{d}\right)\right)$, also significantly improving upon the best-known $\mathcal{O}((d\log T)^{2/3}T^{1/3})$ upper bound and resolving the open problem posed by Cevher et al. [2023], Hait et al. [2025]. Technically, our upper bound for the expert problem is achieved by a corrected variant of Hedge, in which carefully designed correction terms cancel the unfavorable curvature arising in the alternating-regret analysis. We extend the same corrected-potential argument to continuous action sets to obtain the optimal alternating-regret rate for OCO. For the lower bounds, the expert construction repeatedly eliminates half of the candidate experts, while the OCO lower bound instance construction replaces this discrete elimination by a more involved multiscale construction on the unit disk.

</details>


### [189] [Adaptive Regularization for Random Features: A Neighboring Early-Stopping Rule with Oracle-Rate Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25513)
*Caixing Wang,Zhibo Chen,Yue Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向随机特征核岭回归（KRR-RF）的相邻早停自适应正则化选择规则。该方法在逆正则化参数上采用均匀网格，仅比较相邻估计器的差异，相比传统的全对Lepskii型过程大幅减少了差异比较次数。相邻差异及其经验复杂度项可直接在随机特征空间中计算，无需构建精确核Gram矩阵。作者建立了相邻KRR-RF估计器的高概率比较界，并在标准源条件、容量条件及合适的网格和随机特征预算条件下证明所选估计器可达到Oracle多项式学习率（至多相差对数因子）。该方法无需事先知道源和容量指数，覆盖良好设定和部分误设定两种情形，并通过模拟与真实数据实验验证了其预测性能与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征方法为核岭回归（KRR）提供了可扩展的近似方案，但获得Oracle学习率所需的正则化参数依赖于未知的光滑度和容量参数。现有基于Lepskii型的自适应方法需要全对比较，计算开销大，且缺乏针对KRR-RF的高效且理论保证的自适应正则化选择方案。

Method: 提出相邻早停规则：在逆正则化参数上构造均匀网格，仅比较相邻估计器的差异（而非全对比较）。相邻差异和经验复杂度项均在随机特征空间中直接计算，避免构造精确核Gram矩阵。理论分析基于经验随机特征有效维数，将可观测的停止阈值与随机特征模型的总体复杂度联系起来。通过高概率比较界证明所选估计器达到Oracle学习率，并覆盖良好设定与部分误设定两种情形。

Result: 在标准源条件、容量条件以及合适的网格和随机特征预算条件下，所选估计器可达到Oracle多项式学习率（至多相差对数因子），且无需先验知道源和容量指数。相邻比较策略相比全对Lepskii型过程减少了差异比较次数，降低了计算开销。模拟和真实数据实验验证了所提方法在预测性能和计算行为方面的优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机特征核岭回归提供了计算高效且具有理论保证的自适应正则化选择方法。通过相邻早停策略，在保持Oracle学习率的同时显著降低了计算复杂度，并适用于良好设定和部分误设定场景，为大规模核方法的实际应用提供了实用且可靠的正则化调参方案。

Abstract: Random feature methods provide a scalable approximation to kernel ridge regression (KRR), but the regularization parameter that yields the oracle learning rate depends on unknown smoothness and capacity parameters. In this work, we propose a neighboring early-stopping rule for adaptive regularization in KRR with random features (KRR-RF). The method uses a grid that is uniform in inverse regularization and compares only adjacent estimators, reducing the number of discrepancy comparisons relative to standard all-pairs Lepskii-type procedures. Both the neighboring discrepancy and its empirical complexity term can be computed directly in the random feature space, without constructing the exact kernel Gram matrix.
  We establish a high-probability comparison bound for neighboring KRR-RF estimators and show that, under standard source and capacity conditions together with suitable grid and random feature budget conditions, the selected estimator attains the oracle polynomial learning rate up to logarithmic factors. The result allows the regularization parameter to be selected without prior knowledge of the source and capacity exponents and covers both well-specified and partially misspecified regimes. Our analysis is based on an empirical random feature effective dimension that connects the observable stopping threshold with the population complexity of the random feature model. Simulation and real-data experiments illustrate the prediction performance and computational behavior of the proposed method in comparison with standard tuning procedures.

</details>


### [190] [Fast rates in Bayesian online learning with approximate posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25706)
*Ilsang Ohn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了计算近似是否保留精确贝叶斯预测的统计保证（快速遗憾界）。核心结论是：近似后验与精确后验之间的Wasserstein距离以及精确Gibbs后验的收缩半径共同决定了累计近似代价。定理表明，只要近似后验足够精确地追踪精确后验，就能继承快速遗憾界（至多增加一个由近似误差决定的加性项）。并通过三个实例验证：线性模型的投影Langevin算法、Sobolev椭球上的无穷维指数族序列模型的保先验截断方法、以及高斯过程回归的稀疏变分后验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确贝叶斯预测虽享有快速的预测遗憾保证，但精确后验更新或表示在在线场景中计算代价过高，无法直接使用。因此需要研究计算近似是否以及何时能保持这些统计保证，为实用化近似贝叶斯方法提供理论基础。

Method: 通过理论分析，建立了完备的定理框架：将精确Gibbs后验的收缩半径与近似后验和精确后验之间的Wasserstein距离纳入统一分析，证明只要近似后验以足够精度追踪精确后验，就可继承快速遗憾界，附带由近似误差决定的加性项。并针对三类模型开发了具体的近似后验算法：投影Langevin算法（线性模型）、保先验截断法（无穷维指数族）、稀疏变分后验（GP回归）。

Result: 三个实例均获得正面结果：投影Langevin算法在对数遗憾界下工作；保先验截断法以亚线性内存和常数更新代价达到极小极大预测遗憾率；稀疏变分后验以显著更低计算成本获得与精确GP相同的预测遗憾率。

Conclusion: 计算近似不一定牺牲贝叶斯预测的统计最优性：只要近似误差受控（以Wasserstein距离度量），即可保留快速遗憾保证。这为设计高效且统计上可靠的近似贝叶斯在线学习方法提供了统一理论框架，并展示了多个实际可行的高效算法。

Abstract: Exact Bayes prediction enjoys fast predictive regret guarantees, but exact posterior updating or representation may be too costly for online use. We study when these statistical guarantees are preserved by computational approximations. We show that the cumulative price of posterior approximation can be governed by the interaction between the contraction radius of the exact Gibbs posterior and the Wasserstein distance between the approximate and exact posteriors. Our general theorem shows that whenever exact Bayes prediction achieves a fast regret bound, any approximate posterior method that tracks the exact posterior with sufficient accuracy inherits the same fast regret, up to an additive term determined by the approximation error. Three online learning examples are developed. For linear models with strongly convex regularized losses, a projected Langevin algorithm yields an approximate posterior that achieves logarithmic regret. For an infinite-dimensional canonical exponential family sequence model over a Sobolev ellipsoid, a prior-preserving truncation method attains the minimax predictive regret rate with sublinear memory and constant update cost per observation. For random-design Gaussian process (GP) regression, a sparse variational posterior with inducing variables achieves the same predictive regret rate as the exact GP, but at substantially lower computational cost.

</details>


### [191] [Multi-output Gaussian process prediction of physical fields under linear equality constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25709)
*Mahamat Hamdan Nassouradine,Clément Gauchy,Pierre-Emmanuel Angeli,Sébastien da Veiga*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于联合建模受线性等式约束的高维多物理场数据的鲁棒框架。该框架首先采用一种名为row-wise PCA的特定PCA方法，该方法具有在潜空间中保留物理约束的特性；然后在潜空间上使用基于特定核参数化的线性约束多输出高斯过程回归。通过人口动力学和工业CFD应用（Reynolds应力张量预测）验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多物理场预测中，高斯过程回归面临两个主要挑战：输出场的高维性，以及预测中物理约束的强制执行。现有常见的"推导法"（从约束关系中用一个输出推导另一个）对任意选择哪个输出进行推导高度敏感，影响预测精度和不确定性量化，因此需要一种对全部字段对称对待且严格满足物理约束的方法。

Method: 该框架分两步：(1) 首先提出row-wise PCA用于多场数据降维，该方法在潜空间中保持物理约束（标准PCA策略无法做到，文中从理论上研究了row-wise选择的optimality）；(2) 在row-wise PCA的潜空间上，采用基于特定核参数化的线性约束多输出GP进行回归。

Result: 在人口动力学问题和一个工业CFD应用（在不可压缩性约束下预测Reynolds应力张量分量）上验证了所提出的框架。

Conclusion: 通过基准实验表明传统推导法对输出选择敏感，而所提出的对称且受约束的row-wise PCA + 线性约束多输出GP框架能够在严格尊重物理约束的同时对所有字段对称建模，且该方法在人口动力学和工业CFD应用中均得到验证。

Abstract: We address the simultaneous prediction of multiple high-dimensional physical fields governed by linear equality constraints, a setting that arises in many real-world applications in physics machine learning. Gaussian process (GP) regression is a widely used surrogate modeling approach due to its effectiveness in small-sample regimes and its ability to provide uncertainty quantification. However, applying GP models in this setting raises two major challenges: the high dimensionality of the discretized output fields and the enforcement of the physical constraint in predictions. For the latter, a common strategy consists in deducing one output from the others via the constraint relation. Through a benchmark, we show that this deductive approach is sensitive to the arbitrary choice of which output to deduce, affecting both predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification. Consequently, there is a need for an approach that treats all fields symmetrically while strictly respecting the underlying physics. Motivated by these limitations, we propose a robust framework for jointly modeling constrained multi-field data. Our approach first leverages a specific PCA procedure for multi-field data, coined row-wise PCA, which has the interesting property of preserving the constraint in the latent space. Since standard PCA strategies for multi-field data do not preserve such constraints, we investigate theoretically the optimality of the row-wise choice. In a second step, we consider a linearly-constrained multi-output GP approach based on a specific kernel parametrization which is trained on the latent space of row-wise PCA. The proposed framework is validated on a population dynamics problem and on an industrial CFD application, which involves the prediction of Reynolds stress tensor components under the incompressibility constraint.

</details>


### [192] [Efficient Estimation of High Information Projections using Nearest Neighbours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25887)
*David P. Hofmeyr*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最近邻关系增强的直觉性降维方法，通过对编码局部协方差结构的矩阵进行谱分解得到投影方向。该矩阵在标准正则条件下是 Density Information Matrix (DIM) 的一致估计量，后者是 Fisher Information Matrix 的非参数类比，与独立成分分析及充分降维问题紧密相关。与现有 DIM 估计器相比，本方法计算高效且直接估计真实密度而非代理密度，并展示了其在聚类分析和异常检测下游任务中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DIM 估计器计算代价高昂，且只能估计与真实密度平方成比例的代理密度的 DIM，而非真实密度的 DIM，这限制了该方法在实际应用中的可行性。

Method: 所提出的投影源于一个矩阵的谱分解，该矩阵旨在编码数据中的局部协方差结构，其中每个点的局部协方差由该点的最近邻对捕获。利用最近邻关系增强，在标准正则性条件下该矩阵是 DIM 的一致估计量，从而为谱分解提供了直接且高效的途径。

Result: 证明了该矩阵是 DIM 的一致估计量，克服了先前估计器目标代理密度（真实密度平方）的局限；并在聚类分析和异常检测等下游任务中验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法为降维提供了一种计算高效且理论上完备的途径，能发现多变量数据中有趣的投影方向，并在下游任务中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: An intuitive method for dimensionality reduction is proposed, which is highly effective for finding interesting projections of multivariate data. Following similar intuitive motivation to a number of existing techniques, the proposed method is based on enhancing the nearest neighbour relationships in the data. The proposed projection arises from the spectral decomposition of a matrix designed to encode the local covariance structure in the data, where the local covariance at a point is captured by pairs of its nearest neighbours. We show that under standard regularity conditions this matrix is a consistent estimator of the so-called ``Density Information Matrix'' (DIM); a non-parametric analogue of the Fisher Information Matrix. Spectral decompositions of DIMs have been shown to be connected with the important problems of Independent Components Analysis and, in the supervised context, Sufficient Dimension Reduction. However, existing estimators of the DIM are computationally expensive to compute and only target the DIM of a surrogate density, which is proportional to the square of the true underlying density. In addition, we go on to explore the practical utility of our method in aiding the downstream tasks of cluster analysis and outlier detection.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>
